## Less Than 1.25 Kg

### Less Than 1.25 Kg — phần 1

• Give feeds and fluid as described for a well baby less than 1.25 kg (page F-27).

## Weight Gain And Feeding After Seven Days Of Age

### Weight Gain And Feeding After Seven Days Of Age — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; It is normal for small babies to lose weight during the first 7 to 10 days

of life. Birth weight is usually regained by 14 days of life unless the baby has been sick.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby’s growth (page C-53) to ensure that the baby is gaining

weight adequately.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby still requires feeding using an alternative feeding method

but is on full milk feeds:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Increase the volume of milk in increments of 20 ml/kg body weight

per day until the baby reaches 180 ml/kg body weight of breast milk per day;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Continue to increase the volume of milk as the baby’s weight

increases to maintain a volume of 180 ml/kg body weight of breast milk per day.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If weight gain is inadequate (less than 15 g/kg body weight per day

over three days):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Increase the volume of milk to 200 ml/kg body weight per day;

### Weight Gain And Feeding After Seven Days Of Age — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If weight gain is inadequate for more than one week and the

baby has been taking 200 ml/kg body weight breast milk per day, treat for inadequate weight gain (page F-96).

## Kangaroo Mother Care

### Kangaroo Mother Care — phần 1

Kangaroo mother care (KMC) is care of a small baby who is continuously carried in skin-to-skin contact by the mother and exclusively breastfed (ideally). It is the best way to keep a small baby warm and it also helps establish breastfeeding. KMC can be started in the hospital as soon as the baby’s condition permits (i.e. the baby does not require special treatment, such as oxygen or IV fluid). KMC, however, requires that the mother stay with the baby or spend most of the day at the hospital.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the mother is fully recovered from any childbirth

complications before she begins KMC.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Small baby; F-31

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the mother has support from her family to stay at the hospital

or return when the baby is ready for KMC and to deal with responsibilities at home. Discuss with the family, if possible, how they can support the mother so she can provide KMC.

### Kangaroo Mother Care — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the mother that KMC may be the best way for her to care for

her baby once the baby’s condition permits:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the baby will be warm;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the baby will feed more easily;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; episodes of apnoea will be less frequent.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take the baby away from the mother only to change napkins (diapers),

bathe, and assess for clinical findings according to the hospital’s schedule, or as necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Babies can be cared for using KMC until they are about 2.5 kg or 40 weeks

post-menstrual age.

## Beginning Kmc

### Beginning Kmc — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; While the baby is recovering from an illness, the mother can begin to

hold the baby in skin-to-skin contact for short periods of time (one to three hours at a time).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby’s condition is stable and the baby does not require special

treatment (e.g. oxygen or IV fluid), the mother can begin continuous KMC.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the baby is ready for KMC, arrange with the mother a time that is

convenient for her. Ask her to wear light, loose clothing that is comfortable in the ambient temperature, provided the clothing can accommodate the baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the room is at least 25 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; While the mother is holding the baby, describe to her each step of KMC,

demonstrate them, and then allow her to go through the steps herself.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clothe the baby with a pre-warmed shirt open at the front, a napkin, a

hat, and socks.

### Beginning Kmc — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby on the mother’s chest:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the baby in an upright position directly against the mother’s

skin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-32; Small baby

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the baby’s hips and elbows are flexed into a frog-like

position and the baby’s head and chest are on the mother’s chest, with the head in a slightly extended position.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby on the mother’s chest under the mother’s clothes

(Fig. F-2) and cover with a pre-warmed blanket:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Special garments are not needed as long as the mother’s clothes keep

the baby firmly and comfortably in contact with her skin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use a soft piece of fabric (about 1 square metre), folded diagonally

### Beginning Kmc — phần 3

in two and secured with a knot. Make sure it is tied firmly enough to prevent the baby from sliding out if the mother stands, but not so tightly that it obstructs the baby’s breathing or movement.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After positioning the baby, allow the mother to rest with the baby, and

encourage her to move around when she is ready.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-2; Baby in kangaroo mother care position under mother’s

clothes

## Breastfeeding

### Breastfeeding — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother attempt to breastfeed either when the baby is waking

from sleep or when awake and alert.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother sit comfortably, and help her with correct positioning

and attachment (page C-12), if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby cannot be breastfed, have the mother give expressed breast

milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Small baby; F-33

## Daily Life For The Mother

### Daily Life For The Mother — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Emphasize to the mother that it is important that she wash her hands

frequently.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; During the day, the mother can do whatever she likes: she can walk,

stand, sit, or lie down.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The best sleeping position for the mother during KMC is a reclining

position. If the mother’s bed is not adjustable, she can use several pillows to prop herself up. She may also sleep on her side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the mother needs time away from the baby for hygiene or for any

other reason, either:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; have a family member carry the baby in skin-to-skin contact while

the mother is unavailable; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; dress the baby, place in a warm bed, and cover until the mother or a

family member is available to carry the baby in skin-to-skin contact.

## Monitoring The Baby’S Condition

### Monitoring The Baby’S Condition — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is in continuous KMC, measure the baby’s temperature

twice daily.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Teach the mother to observe the baby’s breathing pattern, and explain the

normal variations. If the baby stops breathing, have the mother stimulate the baby to breathe by rubbing the baby’s back for 10 seconds. If the baby does not begin to breathe immediately, resuscitate the baby using a bag and mask (page P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Teach the mother to recognize danger signs (e.g. apnoea, decreased

movement, lethargy, or poor feeding).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Respond to any concerns the mother may have. If the baby is feeding

poorly, determine if the mother’s technique is incorrect (page C-12), the baby is still too immature, or the baby is becoming ill (repeat examination, Table F-2 [page F-11], if necessary).

## Discharge And Follow-Up

### Discharge And Follow-Up — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67). This may be in a few

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-34; Small baby

days to weeks, depending on the initial size of the baby and other problems the baby may have.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the mother is comfortable with her ability to care for the baby

and continue KMC at home and is able to come regularly for follow-up visits.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; During the first week after discharge from the hospital, weigh the baby

daily, if possible, and discuss any problems with the mother. Provide support and encouragement to the mother.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the first week, see the mother and the baby twice weekly until

### Discharge And Follow-Up — phần 2

around 40 weeks post-menstrual age or when the baby weighs more than 2.5 kg. Weigh the baby and advise the mother to begin to wean the baby off KMC as soon as the baby becomes less tolerant of the position.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby is weaned from KMC, continue to follow up monthly to

monitor feeding, growth, and development until the baby is several months old.

## Asphyxia)

### Asphyxia) — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the following points in mind regarding multiple findings:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; A single health problem in a baby may be indicated by many signs

(e.g. a baby with asphyxia may have convulsions, poor feeding, and breathing difficulty), while a single sign may point to many health problems (e.g. poor feeding may be a sign of sepsis, asphyxia, or small size);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Even when multiple signs point to a single problem, the signs

themselves often need to be treated; therefore, it is necessary to look for further guidance in the chapter corresponding to each sign that the baby has. For example, if a baby with asphyxia or sepsis is having both breathing difficulty and convulsions, ensure that management includes treatment for both problems.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Problems that typically produce multiple signs include asphyxia, sepsis,

### Asphyxia) — phần 2

and small size (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation). Congenital syphilis, a specific kind of sepsis, may also produce multiple findings, which are described below. Remember:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; There is considerable overlap between the signs of asphyxia and

sepsis, yet distinguishing between them is important for their appropriate management;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Babies who are small may present with multiple signs, some of

which overlap with the signs of asphyxia and sepsis;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Congenital syphilis is much rarer than the other three problems, even

in areas where the prevalence of sexually transmitted infections is high.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; More than one finding was identified upon assessment, increasing the

likelihood that the baby has a problem related to small size or has sepsis, asphyxia, or congenital syphilis.

### Asphyxia) — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A single finding was identified upon assessment, but the baby later

developed additional signs of illness.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-36; Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia)

(Table F-2, page F-11), looking especially for a history of:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Complicated or difficult labour or birth (e.g. fetal distress);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Prolonged labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Failure of baby to spontaneously breathe at birth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Resuscitation of baby at birth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Maternal uterine infection or fever any time from the onset of labour

to three days after birth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Rupture of the mother’s membranes for more than 18 hours before

birth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Small baby (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks

gestation);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Positive serologic test for syphilis during pregnancy or after birth;

### Asphyxia) — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Syphilis (of the mother or partner) during pregnancy. If the mother

or her partner had syphilis during pregnancy, ask whether it was untreated, inadequately treated, or if the treatment status is unknown.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose (page P-13). If the blood glucose is less than

45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l), treat for low blood glucose (page F-91). Return to this chapter for further evaluation if the problems persist after treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Categorize the findings (below).

## Categorize The Findings

### Categorize The Findings — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Look for the baby’s findings in the tables below, which list the findings

that are common to asphyxia and sepsis (Table F-9, page F-37) and the findings characteristic of babies who are small or have congenital syphilis (Table F-10, page F-37). If the baby’s findings are common to asphyxia and sepsis, determine which of the baby’s findings are Category A or Category B, and use this information to select appropriate management (page F-38).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia); F-37

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-9; Findings common to sepsis and asphyxia

- Dòng dữ liệu: Category A Findings; Category B Findings

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Breathing difficulty (e.g. respiratory rate more; • Lethargy or floppiness

- Dòng dữ liệu: than 60 or less than 30 breaths per minute,; • Drowsiness or reduced activity

grunting on expiration, chest indrawing, or central • Vomiting (favours sepsis)

### Categorize The Findings — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: cyanosis [blue tongue and lips]); • Abdominal distension

• Failure of baby to spontaneously breathe at birth, • Poor or no feeding after having

- Dòng dữ liệu: usually requiring resuscitation (favours asphyxia); fed well (favours sepsis)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Apnoea; • Signs starting at birth or on day 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Convulsions; of life (favours asphyxia)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Unconsciousness; • Prolonged labour (favours sepsis)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Abnormal body temperature since birth and not; • Unclean birth (favours sepsis)

- Dòng dữ liệu: responding to treatment, unstable temperature; • Complicated or difficult labour or

- Dòng dữ liệu: after three or more normal temperature readings,; birth (fetal distress; favours

- Dòng dữ liệu: or temperature more than 39 °C not caused by; asphyxia)

### Categorize The Findings — phần 3

overheating (favours sepsis) • Condition worsens rapidly and dramatically (favours sepsis) • Signs starting after day 4 of life (favours sepsis) • Maternal history of uterine infection or fever any time from the onset of labour to three days after birth, or rupture of membranes for more than 18 hours before birth (favours sepsis)

TABLE F-10 Findings characteristic of babies who are small or have congenital syphilis

- Dòng dữ liệu: Findings; Characteristic of

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks; Small baby (see page F-40)

gestation • Body temperature less than 36.5 °C • Apnoea • Respiratory distress syndrome (moderate to severe breathing difficulty starting at birth. The baby’s condition worsens during the first two days of life, does not change for the next one to two days, and begins to improve during the next four to seven days.) • Feeding difficulty • Floppiness

### Categorize The Findings — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Generalized oedema (body swelling); Congenital syphilis (see page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Abdominal distension (from enlarged liver and/or; F-41)

spleen or from fluid in abdomen) • Blistering skin rash on palms and soles • Profuse nasal discharge (“snuffles”)

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-38; Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia)

## Selecting Appropriate Management

### Selecting Appropriate Management — phần 1

Keep in mind the following points when selecting appropriate management for the baby, and particularly when differentiating between sepsis and asphyxia:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Sepsis can appear any time from birth to the end of the newborn period:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Suspect sepsis (below) in babies with multiple findings, especially if

sepsis cannot be ruled out or another diagnosis is not certain;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When sepsis occurs within the first three days of life, it is usually

related to rupture of the mother’s membranes for more than 18 hours before birth or the presence of a uterine infection or fever any time from the onset of labour to three days after birth. If this history is present in addition to clinical signs, suspect sepsis (below);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby did not become ill until day 4 or later, the presence or

### Selecting Appropriate Management — phần 2

absence of maternal history findings no longer helps to distinguish sepsis from other problems. However, if reliable information about the pregnancy, birth, and postnatal period is not available and the baby is more than three days old, suspect sepsis (below);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Asphyxia is associated with a history of difficult or prolonged labour or

birth (e.g. fetal distress) and/or failure of the baby to breathe spontaneously at birth, usually requiring resuscitation. If these events occurred, suspect asphyxia (page F-39).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is small, see page F-40.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has findings characteristic of syphilis and the mother or

her partner has a history of syphilis during the pregnancy or the mother had a positive serologic test for syphilis during pregnancy, suspect congenital syphilis (page F-41).

## If Sepsis Is Suspected

### If Sepsis Is Suspected — phần 1

Review the two main bullets below, and choose the one that is more appropriate based on the baby’s findings. See Table F-11 (page F-40) for a summary of the decision-making pathway to distinguish sepsis from asphyxia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is three days old or less and the maternal history IS

indicative of sepsis, treat for sepsis (page F-41), and treat any specific sign (e.g. breathing difficulty).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia); F-39

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is three days old or less and the maternal history IS NOT

indicative of sepsis OR if the baby was more than three days old when signs of illness were first noted (regardless of maternal history):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has two or more Category A findings OR three or

more Category B findings, treat for sepsis (page F-41), and treat any specific sign (e.g. breathing difficulty);

### If Sepsis Is Suspected — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has one Category A finding and one Category B

finding OR two Category B findings, treat any specific sign (e.g. breathing difficulty) but do not begin treatment for sepsis. Observe the baby for additional signs of sepsis, reassessing the baby every two hours for 12 hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If additional signs of sepsis are found at any time during the

observation period, treat for sepsis (page F-41);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If no additional signs of sepsis are found during the

observation period but the initial signs have not improved, continue observation for 12 more hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the initial signs of sepsis improved during the observation

period, reassess the baby every four hours for an additional 24 hours. If improvement continues, the baby is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## If Asphyxia Is Suspected

### If Asphyxia Is Suspected — phần 1

Review the two main bullets below and choose the one that is more appropriate based on the baby’s findings. See Table F-11 (page F-40) for a summary of the decision-making pathway to distinguish sepsis from asphyxia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is three days old or less and the maternal history IS

indicative of sepsis (see above), treat the baby for both asphyxia (page F-44) and sepsis (page F-41), and treat any specific sign (e.g. breathing difficulty).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is three days old or less and the maternal history IS NOT

indicative of sepsis OR if the baby was more than three days old when signs of illness were first noted (regardless of maternal history), treat for asphyxia (page F-44), and treat any specific sign (e.g. breathing difficulty).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-40; Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia)

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-11; Summary of decision-making pathway to distinguish sepsis

### If Asphyxia Is Suspected — phần 2

from asphyxia

- Dòng dữ liệu: Baby’s Age and; Findings More; Treat for

- Dòng dữ liệu: Mother’s History; Consistent with

- Dòng dữ liệu: Baby three days old or less; Asphyxia; Sepsis (page F-41) and asphyxia

- Dòng dữ liệu: and maternal history; (page F-44)

suggestive of sepsis

- Dòng dữ liệu: Sepsis; Sepsis (page F-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Baby three days old or less; Asphyxia; Asphyxia (page F-44)

and maternal history not suggestive of sepsis

- Dòng dữ liệu: OR; Sepsis; Possible sepsis (review for

- Dòng dữ liệu: Baby became ill on day 4; presence of Category A or B

or later findings; Table F-9, page F-37)

## If The Baby Is Small

### If The Baby Is Small — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is small and has findings characteristic of sepsis or

asphyxia (see Table F-9, page F-37):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Treat for sepsis and/or asphyxia as needed (see above), treat any

specific sign (e.g. breathing difficulty), and provide general care for the small baby (page F-23);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s findings include occasional apnoea, floppiness,

feeding difficulty, and/or breathing difficulty that is consistent with the pattern of respiratory distress syndrome (RDS), with no maternal history suggestive of sepsis:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; See pages F-51 and F-52 for management of RDS and

apnoea, respectively;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not treat for sepsis unless instructed to do so in the

management section for RDS or apnoea;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide general care for the small baby (page F-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby does not have findings characteristic of sepsis or asphyxia:

### If The Baby Is Small — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide general care for the small baby (page F-23);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Note that small babies often develop new findings during the first

days and weeks of life; return to this chapter any time the baby shows multiple findings consistent with sepsis or asphyxia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia); F-41

## If Congenital Syphilis Is Suspected

### If Congenital Syphilis Is Suspected — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has findings characteristic of syphilis OR the mother or

her partner has a history of syphilis during the mother’s pregnancy, perform a serologic test for syphilis on the mother and baby:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother’s or baby’s test for syphilis is positive, treat for

congenital syphilis (page F-46);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the serologic test for syphilis is not possible, the mother was

not treated at least 30 days before giving birth or was treated inadequately, or if the mother’s treatment status is unknown, treat for congenital syphilis (page F-46).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother had a positive serologic test for syphilis during

pregnancy, determine if the mother was treated:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother was treated adequately and the treatment started

at least 30 days before birth, no treatment is necessary;

### If Congenital Syphilis Is Suspected — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother was not treated at least 30 days before giving birth

or was treated inadequately, or if the mother’s treatment status is unknown, treat for congenital syphilis (page F-46).

## Sepsis

### Sepsis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22) for the first 12 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9), and send it to the laboratory for culture

and sensitivity, if possible, and to measure haemoglobin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%),

give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has convulsions, opisthotonos, or a bulging anterior

fontanelle, suspect meningitis:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Treat convulsions, if present (page F-59);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Perform a lumbar puncture (page P-37);

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-42; Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Send a sample of the cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) to the laboratory for

cell count, Gram stain, culture, and sensitivity;

### Sepsis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Begin treatment for meningitis (page F-43) while awaiting

laboratory confirmation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If meningitis is not suspected, give ampicillin and gentamicin IV

according to the baby’s age and weight (Table C-9, pages C-34 to C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby’s condition every six hours for signs of improvement:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s condition is improving after three days of

treatment with antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is negative, discontinue ampicillin and

gentamicin after five days of treatment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is not possible or is positive, continue

ampicillin and gentamicin to complete 10 days of treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s condition is not improving after three days of

treatment with antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is positive, change antibiotics according to

### Sepsis — phần 3

the results of the culture and sensitivity, and give antibiotics for seven days after signs of improvement are first noted;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is not possible or the organism cannot be

identified, discontinue ampicillin. Give cefotaxime IV according to the baby’s age (Table C-9, page C-34), in addition to gentamicin, for seven days after signs of improvement are first noted.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After 12 hours of treatment with antibiotics or when the baby’s condition

begins to improve, allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure haemoglobin twice weekly during hospitalization and again at

discharge. If the haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

### Sepsis — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If signs of sepsis recur, repeat the culture and sensitivity, and treat

with additional antibiotics as necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia); F-43

## Meningitis

### Meningitis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a lumbar puncture (page P-37) if not already done (see page

F-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give ampicillin (for meningitis) and gentamicin IV according to the baby’s

age and weight (Table C-9, pages C-34 to C-35). Note that the dose of ampicillin given for meningitis is double the dose given for sepsis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm the diagnosis of meningitis if the:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; white blood cell count in the cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) is 20/mm3 or

more if the baby is less than seven days old, or 10/mm3 or more if the baby is seven days or older; OR

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; culture or Gram stain of the CSF is positive.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After 12 hours of treatment with antibiotics or when the baby’s condition

begins to improve, allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

### Meningitis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s condition is improving after 48 hours of treatment with

antibiotics, continue antibiotics for 14 days or for seven days after signs of improvement are first noted, whichever is longer.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s condition is not improving after 48 hours of treatment

with antibiotics, repeat the lumbar puncture:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If organisms are seen on Gram stain of the CSF, change

antibiotics according to the organism identified, and treat for 14 days or for seven days after signs of improvement are first noted, whichever is longer;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the organism cannot be identified, discontinue ampicillin. Give

cefotaxime (for meningitis) IV according to the baby’s age (Table C-9, page C-34), in addition to gentamicin, for 14 days or for seven days after signs of improvement are first noted, whichever is longer.

### Meningitis — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure haemoglobin every three days during hospitalization and again

at discharge. If the haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-44; Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If signs of sepsis recur, repeat the culture and sensitivity, and treat

with additional antibiotics as necessary.

## Asphyxia

### Asphyxia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If convulsions occur, treat promptly (page F-64) to prevent worsening

of the baby’s condition.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat breathing difficulty, if present (page F-47).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Classify the severity of asphyxia:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In mild cases of asphyxia, the baby may be jittery or hyperalert, with

increased muscle tone, poor feeding, and a normal or rapid respiratory rate. These findings typically last for 24 to 48 hours before resolving spontaneously;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In moderate cases, the baby may be lethargic and have feeding

difficulty. The baby may have occasional episodes of apnoea and/or convulsions for a few days. These problems usually resolve within one week, but long-term neurodevelopmental problems are possible;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In severe cases, the baby may be floppy or unconscious and does not

### Asphyxia — phần 2

feed. Convulsions may occur for several days, and severe and frequent episodes of apnoea are common. The baby may improve over several weeks or may not improve at all; if these babies survive, they usually suffer permanent brain damage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the asphyxia is mild:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is not receiving oxygen, allow the baby to begin

breastfeeding (page C-11);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is receiving oxygen or otherwise cannot be breastfed,

give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide ongoing care (page F-45).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the asphyxia is moderate or severe:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid for the first

12 hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Restrict the volume of fluid to 60 ml/kg body weight for the first

day, and monitor urine output;

### Asphyxia — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia); F-45

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby urinates less than six times daily or produces no

urine:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not increase the volume of fluid on the next day;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When the amount of urine begins to increase, increase the

volume of IV fluid daily according to the progression of fluid volumes in Table C-4, page C-22, regardless of the baby’s day of age (i.e. for a four-day-old baby, progress from 60 ml/kg to 80 ml/kg to 100 ml/kg, etc.; do not go directly to 120 ml/kg on the first day).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once the convulsions are controlled and the baby shows signs of

increased responsiveness, allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide ongoing care (below).

## Ongoing Care Of Babies With Asphyxia

### Ongoing Care Of Babies With Asphyxia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby every two hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature is less than 36.5 °C or more than

37.5 °C, treat for abnormal body temperature (page F-69);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Treat for convulsions (page F-64) or breathing difficulty (page

F-47) as necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother to hold and cuddle the baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is unconscious, lethargic, or floppy, handle and move the

baby gently to prevent injury when the baby’s muscle tone is low. Support the baby’s entire body, especially the head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s condition is not improving after three days, assess again

for signs of sepsis (Table F-9, page F-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s condition does not improve after one week (baby

### Ongoing Care Of Babies With Asphyxia — phần 2

continues to be lethargic, is not breastfeeding or is feeding poorly, or is still having convulsions) but the baby is no longer receiving treatment at the hospital or the treatment can be continued at home, discuss with the mother the possibility of caring for her baby at home.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has not had a convulsion for three days after discontinuing

phenobarbital, the mother is able to feed the baby, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67):

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-46; Multiple findings (most often sepsis or asphyxia)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Discuss with the mother the baby’s prognosis and how to deal with

the problems the baby may have at home;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Follow up in one week, or earlier if the mother notes serious

problems (e.g. feeding difficulty, convulsions);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Help the mother find the best method to feed the baby if the baby is

### Ongoing Care Of Babies With Asphyxia — phần 3

not breastfeeding well. If the baby is feeding slowly, have the mother feed more frequently.

## Congenital Syphilis

### Congenital Syphilis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a lumbar puncture (page P-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the white blood cell count in the cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) to

determine if the central nervous system (CNS) is involved (i.e. white blood cell count in the CSF is more than 25/mm3):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the CNS is involved or the lumbar puncture is not possible or

the CSF is bloody, give benzylpenicillin IV or IM (Table C-9, page C-34) for 10 days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the CNS is not involved, give procaine benzylpenicillin (or

benzathine benzylpenicillin) IM (Table C-9, pages C-34 to C-35) for 10 days. (If an IV line is already in place, give benzylpenicillin IV [Table C-9, page C-34] for 10 days.)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If jaundice is present, explain to the mother that it may take up to three

months to resolve.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the mother and her partner(s) benzathine benzylpenicillin 1.8 g (2.4

### Congenital Syphilis — phần 2

million units) IM as two injections at separate sites.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Refer the mother and her partner(s) for follow-up to a clinic offering

services for sexually transmitted infections.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby remains well, is feeding well, and there are no other problems

requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Follow up in four weeks to examine the baby for growth and signs of

congenital syphilis;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Report the case to authorities, if required.

## Breathing Difficulty F-47

### Breathing Difficulty F-47 — phần 1

Immediately resuscitate the baby using a bag and mask (page P-1) if the baby:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; is not breathing at all, even when stimulated; OR

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; is gasping; OR

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; has a respiratory rate less than 20 breaths per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s respiratory rate is more than 60 breaths per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s respiratory rate is less than 30 breaths per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has central cyanosis (blue tongue and lips).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has chest indrawing (Fig. F-3).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby is grunting on expiration.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has apnoea (spontaneous cessation of breathing for more than

20 seconds).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-3; Chest indrawing

(Table F-2, page F-11), and use the information to classify the breathing difficulty (Table F-12, page F-49).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide general management, below.

### Breathing Difficulty F-47 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-48; Breathing difficulty

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxygen (page C-25) at a moderate flow rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s respiratory rate is less than 30 breaths per minute,

observe the baby carefully. If the respiratory rate is less than 20 breaths per minute at any time, resuscitate the baby using a bag and mask (page P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has apnoea:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Stimulate the baby to breathe by rubbing the baby’s back for 10

seconds;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby does not begin to breathe immediately, resuscitate the

baby using a bag and mask (page P-1);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; See page F-52 for additional management of apnoea.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose (page P-13). If the blood glucose is less than

45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l), treat for low blood glucose (page F-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are any signs in addition to difficulty breathing, see Multiple

### General Management — phần 2

Findings (Most Often Sepsis or Asphyxia) (page F-35) to determine if the baby’s problems are due to small size or if the baby has asphyxia, sepsis, or congenital syphilis, and continue to treat for difficulty breathing.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s respiratory rate is more than 60 breaths per minute and

the baby has central cyanosis (even if receiving oxygen at a high flow rate) but no chest indrawing or grunting on expiration, suspect a congenital heart abnormality (page F-52).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Classify the breathing difficulty as severe, moderate, or mild (Table F-12,

page F-49), and manage accordingly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Breathing difficulty; F-49

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-12; Classification of breathing difficultya

- Dòng dữ liệu: Respiratory Rate; Grunting or Chest; Classification

- Dòng dữ liệu: (breaths per minute); Indrawing

- Dòng dữ liệu: More than 90; Present; Severe

- Dòng dữ liệu: More than 90; Absent; Moderate

### General Management — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: 60 to 90; Present; Moderate

- Dòng dữ liệu: 60 to 90; Absent; Mild

a Respiratory distress syndrome (RDS) results in breathing difficulty, including chest indrawing and grunting, often associated with apnoea. The baby’s condition worsens during the first two days, does not change for the next one to two days, and begins to improve during the next four to seven days. RDS is typically seen in small babies (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation) and starts within hours of birth. If a

small baby with breathing difficulty has findings consistent with this pattern, treat for moderate breathing difficulty due to RDS (page F-51).

## Severe Breathing Difficulty

### Severe Breathing Difficulty — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a gastric tube (page P-33) to empty the stomach of air and

secretions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor and record the baby’s respiratory rate, presence of chest

indrawing or grunting on expiration, and episodes of apnoea every three hours until the baby no longer requires oxygen and then for an additional 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s response to oxygen (page C-29).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the baby begins to show signs of improvement:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When oxygen is no longer needed, allow the baby to begin

breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s breathing difficulty worsens or the baby has central

cyanosis:

### Severe Breathing Difficulty — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-50; Breathing difficulty

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give oxygen (page C-25) at a high flow rate;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If breathing difficulty is so severe that the baby has central

cyanosis even in 100% oxygen, organize transfer (page C-63) and urgently refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre capable of assisted ventilation, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s tongue and lips have remained pink without oxygen for at

least two days, the baby has no difficulty breathing and is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Moderate Breathing Difficulty Not Due To Rds

### Moderate Breathing Difficulty Not Due To Rds — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22) for the first 12 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor and record the baby’s respiratory rate, presence of chest

indrawing or grunting on expiration, and episodes of apnoea every three hours until the baby no longer requires oxygen and then for an additional 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s breathing difficulty does not improve or worsens after

two hours, manage for severe breathing difficulty (page F-49).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s response to oxygen (page C-29).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the baby begins to show signs of improvement:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When oxygen is no longer needed, allow the baby to begin

### Moderate Breathing Difficulty Not Due To Rds — phần 2

breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s tongue and lips have remained pink without oxygen for at

least one day, the baby has no difficulty breathing and is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Breathing difficulty; F-51

## Syndrome

### Syndrome — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22) for the first 12 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor and record the baby’s respiratory rate, presence of chest

indrawing or grunting on expiration, and episodes of apnoea every three hours until the baby no longer requires oxygen and then for an additional 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s condition does not stabilize after two days, manage as

for severe breathing difficulty (page F-49).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s response to oxygen (page C-29).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the baby begins to show signs of improvement:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When oxygen is no longer needed, allow the baby to begin

### Syndrome — phần 2

breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s tongue and lips have remained pink without oxygen for at

least one day, the baby has no difficulty breathing and is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring special treatment, provide routine care for a small baby (page F-23) until the baby is ready to be discharged (page C-67).

## Mild Breathing Difficulty

### Mild Breathing Difficulty — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor and record the baby’s respiratory rate, presence of chest

indrawing or grunting on expiration, and episodes of apnoea every three hours until the baby no longer requires oxygen and then for an additional 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s response to oxygen (page C-29).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When oxygen is no longer needed, allow the baby to begin breastfeeding

(page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, continue giving expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-52; Breathing difficulty

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the breathing difficulty worsens at any time during the

observation period:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has the typical pattern of RDS, treat for moderate

breathing difficulty caused by RDS (page F-51);

### Mild Breathing Difficulty — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby does NOT have the typical pattern of RDS, look for

signs of sepsis (Table F-9, page F-37) and treat if found (page F-41), and manage moderate (page F-50) or severe (page F-49) breathing difficulty as necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s tongue and lips have remained pink without oxygen for at

least one day, the baby has no difficulty breathing and is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Congenital Heart Abnormality

### Congenital Heart Abnormality — phần 1

The diagnosis of a heart abnormality is made by exclusion of other diagnoses.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxygen (page C-25) at a high flow rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby cannot tolerate feeding, establish an IV line (page P-21),

and give IV fluid at maintenance volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Organize transfer (page C-63), and refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or

specialized centre for further evaluation, if possible.

## Small Baby

### Small Baby — phần 1

Small babies are prone to episodes of apnoea, which are more frequent in very small babies (less than 1.5 kg at birth or born before 32 weeks gestation) but they become less frequent as the baby grows.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Teach the mother to observe the baby closely for further episodes of

apnoea. If the baby stops breathing, have the mother stimulate the baby to breathe by rubbing the baby’s back for 10 seconds. If the baby does not begin to breathe immediately, resuscitate the baby using a bag and mask (page P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Breathing difficulty; F-53

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review the general principles of feeding and fluid management of small

babies (page F-26).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the use of kangaroo mother care (page F-30), if possible.

Babies cared for in this way have fewer apnoeic episodes, and the mother is able to observe the baby closely.

### Small Baby — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the apnoeic episodes become more frequent, treat for sepsis (page

F-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has not had an episode of apnoea for seven days, is feeding

well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Term Baby

### Term Baby — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a term baby has had only a single episode of apnoea:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Observe the baby closely for further episodes of apnoea for 24

hours, and teach the mother how to do so. If the baby does not have another apnoeic episode in 24 hours, is feeding well, and has no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If apnoea recurs, manage for multiple episodes of apnoea, below.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a term baby has had multiple episodes of apnoea:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Treat for sepsis (page F-41);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has not had an episode of apnoea in the past seven

days, is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-54; Breathing difficulty

## Before Birth

### Before Birth — phần 1

A baby born to a mother with a uterine infection or fever any time from the onset of labour to three days after birth, or rupture of membranes for more than 18 hours before birth, is often normal at birth but can develop a problem later. The management suggested in this chapter is intended to prevent sepsis from occurring in a baby who does not have signs of illness at birth. If the baby has ANY sign, follow the appropriate sign chapter. If a baby has multiple findings

of illness (e.g. breathing difficulty, vomiting, and lethargy), see the chapter Multiple Findings (Most Often Sepsis or Asphyxia) (page F-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s mother has or had a uterine infection or fever any time from

the onset of labour to three days after birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s mother’s membranes were ruptured for more than 18 hours

before birth.

## Management

### Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is more than three days old (regardless of gestational age),

no treatment (e.g. antibiotics) or observation is needed. Explain to the mother the signs of sepsis (e.g. Table F-9, page F-37), and ask her to return with the baby if the baby develops any signs of sepsis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is three days old or less, treat according to gestational age

(below).

## Membranes

### Membranes — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41) with the following modifications:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is positive or if the baby develops signs of

sepsis, continue antibiotics to complete 10 days of treatment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-56; Mother with history of uterine infection or fever during labour or after birth,

or rupture of membranes for more than 18 hours before birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is negative and the baby still has no signs of

sepsis after five days of treatment with antibiotics, discontinue antibiotics;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is not possible but the baby still has no signs

of sepsis after five days of treatment with antibiotics, discontinue antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby remains well, is feeding well, and there are no other

### Membranes — phần 2

problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain to the mother the signs of sepsis (e.g. Table F-9, page F-37),

and ask her to return with the baby if the baby develops any signs of sepsis.

## Rupture Of Membranes Without Uterine Infection Or Fever

### Rupture Of Membranes Without Uterine Infection Or Fever — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9), and send it to the laboratory for culture

and sensitivity, if possible, but do not start antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe for signs of sepsis (e.g. poor feeding, vomiting, breathing

difficulty; Table F-9, page F-37) every four hours for 48 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the blood culture is negative, the baby still has no signs of sepsis after

48 hours and is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain to the mother the signs of sepsis (e.g. Table F-9, page

F-37), and ask her to return with the baby if the baby develops any signs of sepsis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the blood culture is positive or if the baby develops signs of sepsis,

treat for sepsis (page F-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the blood culture is not possible, observe the baby for an additional

### Rupture Of Membranes Without Uterine Infection Or Fever — phần 2

three days. If the baby remains well during this time, discharge the baby (page C-67). Explain to the mother the signs of sepsis (e.g. Table F-9, page F-37), and ask her to return with the baby if the baby develops any signs of sepsis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Mother with history of uterine infection or fever during labour or after birth,; F-57

or rupture of membranes for more than 18 hours before birth

## Or More Early) Or Birth Weight Less Than 2 Kg

### Or More Early) Or Birth Weight Less Than 2 Kg — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41) with the following modifications:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is positive or if the baby develops signs of

sepsis, continue antibiotics to complete 10 days of treatment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is negative and the baby still has no signs of

sepsis after five days of treatment with antibiotics, discontinue antibiotics;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood culture is not possible but the baby still has no signs

of sepsis after five days of treatment with antibiotics, discontinue antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby remains well, is feeding well, and there are no other

problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain to the mother the signs of sepsis (e.g. Table F-9, page F-37),

### Or More Early) Or Birth Weight Less Than 2 Kg — phần 2

and ask her to return with the baby if the baby develops any signs of sepsis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-58; Mother with history of uterine infection or fever during labour or after birth,

or rupture of membranes for more than 18 hours before birth

## Convulsions Or Spasms F-59

### Convulsions Or Spasms F-59 — phần 1

Convulsions can be due to asphyxia, birth injury, or hypoglycaemia and are also a sign of meningitis or neurologic problems. Between the convulsions, the baby may appear normal or may be unconscious, lethargic, or irritable. The spasms of neonatal tetanus may superficially resemble convulsions, but the two need to be distinguished from each other because the treatment of tetanus differs from that of convulsions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby is having a convulsion or spasm.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has a history of convulsions or spasms.

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is currently having a convulsion/spasm, look for

distinguishing features (Table F-13, page F-60).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is not currently having a convulsion/spasm, ask the mother

(or whoever brought the baby in):

### Convulsions Or Spasms F-59 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Can you describe the type of abnormal movements the baby had?

Were they triggered by noise or handling? (Use Table F-13, page F-60 to differentiate between a convulsion and a spasm.)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Did the baby’s condition suddenly deteriorate?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Did the baby suddenly become pale?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Did you receive the complete immunization schedule for tetanus?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Was the birth unclean or were unclean or harmful substances (e.g.

animal dung) applied to the baby’s umbilicus?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Did the baby have jaundice early (on or before day 2 of life)? If so,

was the baby treated?

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose (page P-13). If the blood glucose is less than

45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l), treat for low blood glucose (page F-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-14, page F-62).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-60; Convulsions or spasms

### Convulsions Or Spasms F-59 — phần 3

TABLE F-13 Distinguishing features of convulsions and spasms

- Dòng dữ liệu: Problem; Typical Findings

- Dòng dữ liệu: Generalized; • Repetitive jerking movements of limbs or face

- Dòng dữ liệu: convulsiona; • Continuous extension or flexion of arms and legs, either

synchronous or asynchronous • Apnoea (spontaneous cessation of breathing for more than 20 seconds) • Baby may appear unconscious or awake but unresponsive)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Subtle; • Repetitive blinking, eye deviation, or staring

- Dòng dữ liệu: convulsiona; • Repetitive movements of mouth or tongue

• Purposeless movement of the limbs, as if bicycling or swimming • Apnoea • Baby may be conscious

- Dòng dữ liệu: Spasm; • Involuntary contraction of muscles (Fig. F-4A) that lasts a few

### Convulsions Or Spasms F-59 — phần 4

seconds to several minutes • Fists often persistently and tightly clenched (Fig. F-4A) • Trismus (tight jaw; the baby’s mouth cannot be opened and the lips may be pursed together in a “fish mouth” expression; Fig. F-4A) • Opisthotonos (extreme hyperextension of the body, with the head and heels bent backward and the body arched forward; Fig. F-4B) • Triggered by touch, light, or sound • Baby is conscious throughout, often crying with pain

FIGURE F-4 Babies with spasms of the face and limbs (A) and opisthotonos (B)

a Note that generalized and subtle convulsions are both managed in the same way.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Convulsions or spasms; F-61

Ensure that the baby is having a convulsion or a spasm and is not just jittery:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Like convulsions, jitteriness is characterized by rapid,

repetitive movements; however, in a jittery baby, these movements are of the same amplitude and in the same direction.

### Convulsions Or Spasms F-59 — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Like spasms, jitteriness can be precipitated by sudden

handling of the baby or by loud noises, but it is usually stopped by cuddling, feeding, or flexing the baby’s limb.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-62; Convulsions or spasms

## Initial Management Of Convulsions

### Initial Management Of Convulsions — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22) for the first 12 hours. If the convulsions are due to asphyxia, see page F-44 for guidelines on fluid volume.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s blood glucose was less than 45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l),

ensure that the baby was treated for low blood glucose (page F-91) before continuing with management of the convulsion (below) to rule out hypoglycaemia as the cause of the convulsions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is currently having a convulsion or the baby had a

convulsion within the last hour, give phenobarbital 20 mg/kg body weight IV slowly over five minutes:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If an IV line has not yet been established, give phenobarbital

20 mg/kg body weight as a single IM injection;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If convulsions do not stop within 30 minutes, give another dose of

### Initial Management Of Convulsions — phần 2

phenobarbital 10 mg/kg body weight IV slowly over five minutes (or IM if an IV line still has not yet been established). Repeat one more time after another 30 minutes, if necessary;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If convulsions continue or if they recur within six hours, give

phenytoin 20 mg/kg body weight IV, noting the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give phenytoin IV only;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Mix the total dose of phenytoin in 15 ml of normal saline and

infuse at the rate of 0.5 ml per minute over 30 minutes. Use only normal saline to infuse phenytoin, as all other fluid will cause the phenytoin to crystallize.

## Caution

### Caution — phần 1

Do not use diazepam for convulsions; diazepam given in addition to phenobarbital will increase the risk of circulatory collapse and respiratory failure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has central cyanosis (blue tongue and lips) or other signs

of breathing difficulty, give oxygen (page C-25) at a moderate flow rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Convulsions or spasms; F-65

## Ongoing Care Of Babies Who Have Had Convulsions

### Ongoing Care Of Babies Who Have Had Convulsions — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for recurrence of convulsions, looking especially for

subtle convulsions (Table F-13, page F-60).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If convulsions recur within two days, give phenobarbital 5 mg/kg body

weight once daily by mouth until the baby has not had a convulsion for seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If convulsions recur after two days without convulsions, repeat

treatment with phenobarbital as described for initial management of convulsions (page F-64) and again follow with phenobarbital 5 mg/kg body weight once daily by mouth until the baby has not had a convulsion for seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is receiving daily phenobarbital:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Continue phenobarbital for seven days after the last convulsion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once phenobarbital is discontinued, observe the baby for an

additional three days.

### Ongoing Care Of Babies Who Have Had Convulsions — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue IV fluid at maintenance volume according to the baby’s age

(Table C-4, page C-22). If the convulsions are due to asphyxia, see page F-44 for guidelines on fluid volume.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby’s condition is stable, allow the baby to begin

breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide general care for the baby:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the mother to hold the baby, but avoid overstimulation by

noise and excessive handling;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Handle and move the baby gently to prevent injury when the baby’s

muscle tone is low. Support the baby’s entire body, especially the head;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain to the mother that the phenobarbital will make the baby very

sleepy for several days.

### Ongoing Care Of Babies Who Have Had Convulsions — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the mother that if convulsions stop and the baby feeds well by

seven days of age, recovery will probably be complete.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s condition does not improve after one week (baby

continues to be lethargic, is not breastfeeding or is feeding poorly, or is still having convulsions) but the baby is no longer receiving treatment

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-66; Convulsions or spasms

at the hospital or the treatment can be continued at home, discuss with the mother the possibility of caring for her baby at home.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has not had a convulsion for three days after

discontinuing phenobarbital, the mother is able to feed the baby, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Discuss with the mother the baby’s prognosis and how to deal with

the problems the baby may have at home;

### Ongoing Care Of Babies Who Have Had Convulsions — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Follow up in one week, or earlier if the mother notes serious

problems (e.g. feeding difficulty, convulsions);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Help the mother find the best method to feed the baby if the baby is

not breastfeeding well. If the baby is feeding slowly, have the mother feed more frequently.

## Intraventricular Bleeding

### Intraventricular Bleeding — phần 1

Intraventricular bleeding is difficult to distinguish from meningitis, especially in a baby with other problems during the first days of life. Therefore, treat for meningitis until infection can be ruled out as the cause of convulsions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide general management for bleeding (page F-114).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for convulsions (page F-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for meningitis (page F-43) until infection can be ruled out as the

cause of the convulsions.

## Tetanus

### Tetanus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give diazepam 1 mg/kg body weight IV slowly over three minutes:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If an IV line cannot be established, insert a gastric tube (page P-33),

and give the diazepam via the tube;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Convulsions or spasms; F-67

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If diazepam is not available, give paraldehyde 0.3 ml/kg body

weight in arachis oil rectally (page P-41). Do not give paraldehyde IM or IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the spasms do not stop within 30 minutes, give another dose of

diazepam 1 mg/kg body weight IV slowly over three minutes (or paraldehyde 0.3 ml/kg body weight rectally). Repeat one more time after another 30 minutes, if necessary;

If the baby’s respiratory rate is less than 30 breaths per minute, withhold diazepam, even if the baby continues to have spasms.

### Tetanus — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If spasms continue or if they recur, give additional diazepam

1 mg/kg body weight IV slowly (or by gastric tube if an IV line still has not been established) every six hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has central cyanosis (blue tongue and lips) after the

spasm, give oxygen at a moderate flow rate (page C-25). Use only a head box, as other methods of administering oxygen may cause spasms.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the baby:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; antitetanus immunoglobulin (human) 500 units IM, if available, or

give equine tetanus antitoxin 5000 units IM;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; tetanus vaccine (tetanus toxoid) 0.5 ml IM at a different site from the

immunoglobulin or antitoxin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; benzylpenicillin IV or IM (Table C-9, page C-34) for seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the mother tetanus vaccine (tetanus toxoid) 0.5 ml (to protect her

### Tetanus — phần 3

and any baby she may have in the future), and ask her to return in one month for a second dose.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the umbilicus is red and swollen, draining pus, or foul smelling,

treat for infection of the umbilicus (page F-135).

## Ongoing Care Of Babies With Tetanus

### Ongoing Care Of Babies With Tetanus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Care for the baby in a quiet, darkened room to reduce unnecessary

stimulation, but make sure the baby is not neglected.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue IV fluid at maintenance volume according to the baby’s age

(Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18) between the

spasms. Start with half the volume appropriate for the baby’s age (Table

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-68; Convulsions or spasms

C-4, page C-22), and slowly decrease the volume of IV fluid while increasing the volume of oral feeds over a period of two days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has not had a spasm for two days, has received all doses of

benzylpenicillin, is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Abnormal Body Temperature F-69

### Abnormal Body Temperature F-69 — phần 1

Low body temperature (hypothermia) may be caused by exposure to a cold environment (low ambient temperature, cold surface, or draught), or the baby may be wet or under-dressed for age and size. Elevated body temperature (hyperthermia) may be caused by exposure to a warm environment (e.g. high ambient temperature, sun exposure, or overheating by an incubator or radiant warmer). Hypothermia and hyperthermia may also be signs of illness, such as sepsis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s axillary temperature is less than 36.5 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s axillary temperature is more than 37.5 °C.

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information to determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-15, page F-70).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine if the baby was exposed to a cold or hot environment. Ask the

mother (or whoever brought the baby in):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Was the baby dried after birth and kept warm?

### Abnormal Body Temperature F-69 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Has the baby been clothed appropriately for the climate?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Has the baby been sleeping separately from the mother?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Was the baby exposed to the sun?

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby was being treated under a radiant warmer or was in an

incubator or cot in the hospital when the abnormal body temperature was first noted, check the:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; room temperature;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; temperature setting on the incubator or radiant warmer;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; actual temperature in the incubator or under the radiant warmer;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; frequency with which the baby’s temperature was monitored.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-70; Abnormal body temperature
