## Severe Hypothermia

### Severe Hypothermia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Warm the baby immediately using a prewarmed radiant warmer (page

C-5). Use another method of rewarming (page C-1), if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove cold or wet clothing, if present. Dress the baby in warm clothes

and a hat, and cover with a warm blanket.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41), and keep the tubing of the IV line under the

radiant warmer to warm the fluid.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose (page P-13). If the blood glucose is less than

45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l), treat for low blood glucose (page F-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Look for emergency signs (i.e. respiratory rate less than 20 breaths

per minute, gasping, not breathing, or shock) every hour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure the baby’s temperature every hour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature is increasing at least 0.5 °C per

### Severe Hypothermia — phần 2

hour over the last three hours, rewarming is successful; continue measuring the baby’s temperature every two hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature does not rise or is rising more

slowly than 0.5 °C per hour, ensure that the temperature of the warming device is set correctly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s respiratory rate is more than 60 breaths per minute or

the baby has chest indrawing or grunting on expiration, treat for breathing difficulty (page F-47).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess readiness to feed every four hours until the baby’s temperature is

within the normal range.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby shows signs of readiness to suckle, allow the baby to begin

breastfeeding (page C-11):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using

an alternative feeding method (page C-14);

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-72; Abnormal body temperature

### Severe Hypothermia — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is not able to feed at all, give expressed breast milk by

gastric tube (page C-18) once the baby’s temperature reaches 35 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby’s temperature is normal, measure the temperature every

three hours for 12 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s temperature remains within the normal range,

discontinue measurements.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67). Advise the mother how to keep the baby warm at home.

## Moderate Hypothermia

### Moderate Hypothermia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove cold or wet clothing, if present.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is present, have her rewarm the baby using skin-to-skin

contact (page C-5) if the baby does not have other problems.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is not present or skin-to-skin contact cannot be used:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Dress the baby in warm clothes and a hat, and cover with a warm

blanket;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Warm the baby using a radiant warmer (page C-5). Use another

method of rewarming (page C-1), if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother to breastfeed more frequently. If the baby cannot

be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose (page P-13). If the blood glucose is less than

45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l), treat for low blood glucose (page F-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s respiratory rate is more than 60 breaths per minute or

### Moderate Hypothermia — phần 2

the baby has chest indrawing or grunting on expiration, treat for breathing difficulty (page F-47).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure the baby’s temperature every hour for three hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature is increasing at least 0.5 °C per hour

over the last three hours, rewarming is successful; continue measuring the baby’s temperature every two hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Abnormal body temperature; F-73

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature does not rise or is rising more slowly

than 0.5 °C per hour, look for signs of sepsis (e.g. poor feeding, vomiting, breathing difficulty; Table F-9, page F-37);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once the baby’s temperature is normal, measure the baby’s

temperature every three hours for 12 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature remains within the normal range,

discontinue measurements.

### Moderate Hypothermia — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67). Advise the mother how to keep the baby warm at home.

## Hyperthermia

### Hyperthermia — phần 1

Do not give antipyretic drugs to reduce the baby’s temperature.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s respiratory rate is more than 60 breaths per minute or

the baby has chest indrawing or grunting on expiration, treat for breathing difficulty (page F-47).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the hyperthermia is due to overwarming under a radiant warmer

or in an incubator:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Reduce the temperature setting of the warming device. If the baby is

in an incubator, open the incubator portholes until the temperature of the incubator is within the normal range;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Undress the baby partially or fully for 10 minutes, then dress and

cover the baby;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Observe for signs of sepsis (e.g. poor feeding, vomiting, breathing

difficulty; Table F-9, page F-37) now and repeat when the baby’s temperature is within the normal range;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure the baby’s temperature every hour until it is within the

### Hyperthermia — phần 2

normal range;

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-74; Abnormal body temperature

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure the temperature in the incubator or the temperature under

the radiant warmer every hour, and adjust the temperature setting accordingly;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Review nursing care practices to ensure that the problem does not

happen again;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide ongoing management of hyperthermia (page F-74).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the hyperthermia is due to exposure to a high ambient

temperature or sun exposure:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the baby in a normal temperature environment (25 °C to

28 °C);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Undress the baby partially or fully for 10 minutes, then dress and

cover the baby;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature is more than 39 °C:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Sponge the baby or give the baby a bath for 10 to 15 minutes in

water that is about 4 °C lower than the baby’s current temperature;

### Hyperthermia — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not use cold water or water that is more than 4 °C lower than

the baby’s temperature;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure the baby’s temperature every hour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature is still abnormal after two hours, treat

for sepsis (page F-41);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide ongoing management of hyperthermia (below).

## Ongoing Management Of Hyperthermia

### Ongoing Management Of Hyperthermia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby receives adequate food or fluid:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby

cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of dehydration (sunken eyes or fontanelle, loss of

skin elasticity, or dry tongue or mucous membranes):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give IV fluid at

maintenance volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22);

- Dòng dữ liệu: Abnormal body temperature; F-75

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Increase the volume of fluid by 10% of the baby’s body weight

on the first day that the dehydration is noted.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose (page P-13). If the blood glucose is less than

45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l), treat for low blood glucose (page F-91).

### Ongoing Management Of Hyperthermia — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby’s temperature is within the normal range, measure the

baby’s temperature every three hours for 12 hours. If the baby’s temperature remains within the normal range, discontinue measurements.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67). Advise the mother how to keep the baby warm at home and protect from overheating.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-76; Abnormal body temperature

## Jaundice F-77

### Jaundice F-77 — phần 1

Many babies, particularly small babies (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation), may have jaundice during the first week of life. In most cases, the level of bilirubin that causes the jaundice is not harmful and does not require treatment. However, any jaundice visible in the first 24 hours of life should be assumed to be serious.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has jaundice.

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information to determine the probable diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother (or whoever brought the baby in):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Did you have a previous baby with early jaundice (on day 1 of life),

haemolytic jaundice, glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase (G6PD) deficiency, or Rhesus (Rh) factor or ABO blood group incompatibility?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; What is your blood group and the blood group of the baby’s father?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Is there a family history of anaemia, enlarged liver, or removal of

spleen?

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Estimate the severity of jaundice:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Observe in good daylight. Jaundice will look more severe if

observed in artificial light and may be missed in poor light;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Lightly press the skin with a finger to reveal the underlying colour of

### Problem — phần 2

the skin and subcutaneous tissue;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Estimate the severity of jaundice by day of life and the area of the

body where jaundice is seen (Table F-16, page F-78).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-78; Jaundice

TABLE F-16 Clinical estimation of severity of jaundice

- Dòng dữ liệu: Age; Jaundice Visible on; Classify as

- Dòng dữ liệu: Day 1; Any visible jaundicea

- Dòng dữ liệu: Day 2; Arms and legsa; Serious jaundice

- Dòng dữ liệu: Day 3 and thereafter; Hands and feet

a Visible jaundice seen anywhere on the body on day 1 or on the hands and feet in addition to the arms and legs on day 2 is very serious and needs to be treated with phototherapy immediately. Do not wait to begin phototherapy until the serum bilirubin level is known.

## Initial Management Of Serious Jaundice

### Initial Management Of Serious Jaundice — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Begin phototherapy (page F-84) if jaundice is classified as serious in

Table F-16.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine if the baby has the following risk factors: less than 2.5 kg at

birth, born before 37 weeks gestation, haemolysis, or sepsis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9), and measure serum bilirubin (if

possible) and haemoglobin, determine the baby’s blood group, and perform a Coombs test:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the serum bilirubin is below the level requiring phototherapy

(Table F-17, page F-79), discontinue phototherapy;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the serum bilirubin is at or above the level requiring

phototherapy (Table F-17, page F-79), continue phototherapy;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the Rh factor and ABO blood group do not indicate a cause of

haemolysis or if there is a family history of G6PD deficiency, obtain a G6PD screen, if possible.

### Initial Management Of Serious Jaundice — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-18, page F-80).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Jaundice; F-79

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-17; Treatment of jaundice based on serum bilirubin level

- Dòng dữ liệu: Phototherapy; Exchange Transfusiona

Age

- Dòng dữ liệu: Baby; Factorb; Baby; Factor

- Dòng dữ liệu: mg/dl; µmol/l; mg/dl; µmol/l; mg/dl; µmol/l; mg/dl; µmol/l

- Dòng dữ liệu: Day 1; Any visible jaundicec; 15; 260; 13; 220

- Dòng dữ liệu: Day 2; 15; 260; 13; 220; 25; 425; 15; 260

- Dòng dữ liệu: Day 3; 18; 310; 16; 270; 30; 510; 20; 340

- Dòng dữ liệu: Day 4; 20; 340; 17; 290; 30; 510; 20; 340

### Initial Management Of Serious Jaundice — phần 3

and thereafter a Exchange transfusion is not described in this guide. These serum bilirubin levels are included in case exchange transfusion is possible or in case the baby can be transferred quickly and safely to another facility where exchange transfusion can be performed. If exchange transfusion is possible and the serum bilirubin has reached the level in this table, send a sample of the mother’s and the baby’s blood. b

Risk factors include small size (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation), haemolysis, and sepsis. Visible jaundice anywhere on the body on day 1 or on the hands and feet in addition to the arms and legs on day 2 is very serious and needs to be treated with phototherapy immediately. Do not wait to begin phototherapy until the serum bilirubin level is known.

## Differential Diagnosis

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

• Floppiness a The diagnosis cannot be made if a finding listed in bold is absent. The presence of a finding listed in bold, however, does not guarantee the diagnosis. The diagnosis is definitively confirmed if a finding listed in italics is present. Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot be used to rule out the diagnosis.

## Haemolytic Jaundice

### Haemolytic Jaundice — phần 1

Haemolytic jaundice in a newborn is most commonly caused by Rh factor or ABO blood group incompatibility between the baby and mother or by G6PD deficiency in the baby. The treatment below applies to all cases of haemolytic jaundice, regardless of the cause.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the serum bilirubin is at a level requiring phototherapy (Table F-17,

page F-79), continue phototherapy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If referral for exchange transfusion is possible:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the serum bilirubin is close to the level requiring exchange

transfusion (Table F-17, page F-79), the haemoglobin is less than 13 g/dl (haematocrit less than 40%), and the Coombs test is positive, urgently refer the baby (see page F-82);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the serum bilirubin cannot be measured and it is not possible to

### Haemolytic Jaundice — phần 2

perform a Coombs test, urgently refer the baby (see page F-82) if jaundice started on day 1 and the haemoglobin is less than 13 g/dl (haematocrit less than 40%);

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-82; Jaundice

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is being referred for exchange transfusion:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Organize transfer (page C-63);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Urgently refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or specialized

centre;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Send a sample of the mother’s and the baby’s blood;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain to the mother why the baby has jaundice, why the

referral is required, and the treatment that the baby will receive.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the mother:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cause of jaundice is Rh factor incompatibility, ensure that

the mother is advised regarding future pregnancies;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has G6PD deficiency, advise the mother about which

### Haemolytic Jaundice — phần 3

substances to avoid to prevent haemolysis in the baby (e.g. anti- malarial drugs, sulfa drugs, aspirin, mothballs, fava beans).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%),

give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If jaundice persists for two weeks or longer in a term baby or three

weeks or longer in a small baby (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation), treat for prolonged jaundice (page F-83).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up after discharge, measuring haemoglobin weekly for four

weeks. If the haemoglobin is less than 8 g/dl (haematocrit less than 24%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

## Jaundice Of Prematurity

### Jaundice Of Prematurity — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the serum bilirubin is at a level requiring phototherapy (Table

F-17, page F-79), continue phototherapy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is less than three days old, observe for jaundice for 24

hours after phototherapy is discontinued.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the jaundice persists for three weeks or longer, treat for prolonged

jaundice (below).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Jaundice; F-83

## Prolonged Jaundice

### Prolonged Jaundice — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discontinue phototherapy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s stools are pale or the urine is dark, organize transfer

(page C-63) and refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre for further evaluation, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother tested positive for syphilis, treat for congenital syphilis

(page F-46).

## Bilirubin Encephalopathy (Kernicterus)

### Bilirubin Encephalopathy (Kernicterus) — phần 1

If severe jaundice is not managed quickly, it may damage the baby’s brain. Initial signs of brain damage are lethargy, floppiness, and poor feeding. After a few days, the baby may develop opisthotonos and a high-pitched cry and have convulsions. The final stage is floppiness and poor feeding. It is difficult to determine whether these signs are a result of the severe jaundice or because of another disease. Therefore, always treat a baby with jaundice even if bilirubin

encephalopathy is suspected.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has a convulsion, manage the convulsion (page F-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the serum bilirubin is at a level requiring phototherapy (Table F-17,

page F-79), continue phototherapy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discuss with the mother the baby’s condition and prognosis:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain the feasibility of exchange transfusion and the likely

prognosis of the baby;

### Bilirubin Encephalopathy (Kernicterus) — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Allow the family to decide if exchange transfusion should be

performed, if it is feasible. If the family requests exchange transfusion, organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre, if possible. Send a sample of the mother’s and the baby’s blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s condition does not improve after one week (baby

continues to be lethargic, is not breastfeeding or is feeding poorly, or is still having convulsions) but the baby is no longer receiving treatment at the hospital or the treatment can be continued at home, discuss with the mother the possibility of caring for her baby at home.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has not had a convulsion for three days after discontinuing

phenobarbital, the mother is able to feed the baby, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67):

### Bilirubin Encephalopathy (Kernicterus) — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-84; Jaundice

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Discuss with the mother the baby’s prognosis and how to deal with

the problems the baby may have at home;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Follow up in one week, or earlier if the mother notes serious

problems (e.g. feeding difficulty, convulsions);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Help the mother find the best method to feed the baby if the baby is

not breastfeeding well. If the baby is feeding slowly, have the mother feed more frequently.

## Preparing The Phototherapy Unit

### Preparing The Phototherapy Unit — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that a plastic cover or shield is in position. This prevents injury to

the baby in case a lamp breaks and helps to screen out harmful ultraviolet light.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Warm the room where the unit is located, if necessary, so that the

temperature under the lights is 28 °C to 30 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Switch on the unit, and ensure that all the fluorescent tubes are working.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Replace fluorescent tubes that are burned out or flickering:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Record the date the tubes were replaced, and measure the total

duration of use of the tubes;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Replace tubes every 2000 hours of use or after three months,

whichever comes first, even if the tubes are still working.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use white linens in the cot, bassinet, or incubator, and place white

### Preparing The Phototherapy Unit — phần 2

curtains around the area where the unit is located to reflect as much light as possible back to the baby (Fig. F-5, page F-85).

## Giving Phototherapy

### Giving Phototherapy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby under the phototherapy lights (Fig. F-5, page F-85):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby weighs 2 kg or more, place the baby naked in the cot or

bassinet. Place or keep smaller babies in an incubator;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the baby as close to the lights as the manufacturer’s

instructions allow;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Jaundice; F-85

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Cover the baby’s eyes with patches, ensuring that the patches do not

block the baby’s nostrils. Do not secure the patches in place with tape.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-5; Baby under phototherapy lights

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Turn the baby every three hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby is fed:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the mother to breastfeed on demand but at least every

three hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; During feeding, remove the baby from the phototherapy unit

and remove the eye patches;

### Giving Phototherapy — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; There is no need to supplement or replace breast milk with any

other type of feed or fluid (e.g. breast-milk substitute, water, sugar water, etc.)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is receiving IV fluid or expressed breast milk,

increase the volume of fluid and/or milk by 10% of the total daily volume per day (Table C-4, page C-22) for as long as the baby is under the phototherapy lights;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is receiving IV fluid or is being fed by gastric tube, do

not remove the baby from the phototherapy lights.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note that the baby’s stool may become loose and yellow while the baby

is receiving phototherapy. This does not require specific treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue other prescribed treatment and tests:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Remove the baby from the phototherapy unit only for procedures

that cannot be performed while under the phototherapy lights;

### Giving Phototherapy — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is receiving oxygen, briefly turn off the lights when

observing the baby for central cyanosis (blue tongue and lips).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-86; Jaundice

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure the baby’s temperature (page P-5) and the temperature of the

air under the lights every three hours. If the baby’s temperature is more than 37.5 °C, adjust the temperature of the room or temporarily remove the baby from the phototherapy unit until the baby’s temperature is 36.5 °C to 37.5 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure serum bilirubin level every 12 hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Discontinue phototherapy when the serum bilirubin level is below

the level at which phototherapy was started (Table F-17, page F-79) or 15 mg/dl (260 µmol/l), whichever is lower;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the serum bilirubin is close to the level requiring exchange

### Giving Phototherapy — phần 4

transfusion (Table F-17, page F-79), organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre for exchange transfusion, if possible. Send a sample of the mother’s and the baby’s blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the serum bilirubin cannot be measured, discontinue phototherapy

after three days.

Bilirubin in the skin rapidly disappears under phototherapy. Skin colour cannot be used as a guide to serum bilirubin level while the baby is receiving phototherapy and for 24 hours after discontinuing phototherapy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After phototherapy has been discontinued:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Observe the baby for 24 hours, and repeat the serum bilirubin

measurement, if possible, or estimate jaundice using the clinical method (Table F-16, page F-78);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If jaundice has returned to or is above the level at which

### Giving Phototherapy — phần 5

phototherapy was started, repeat phototherapy for the same length of time as originally given. Repeat this step each time phototherapy is discontinued until the measured or estimated bilirubin stays below the level requiring phototherapy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If phototherapy is no longer required, the baby is feeding well, and there

are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Teach the mother to assess jaundice, and advise her to return if the baby

becomes more jaundiced.

## Lethargy And Other Non-Specific Signs F-87

### Lethargy And Other Non-Specific Signs F-87 — phần 1

Non-specific signs may be present in a baby with a serious illness. In some cases, the mother may just think the baby “looks ill.” The most common serious problems causing these non-specific signs are sepsis and asphyxia. Lethargy, drowsiness, and reduced activity may also be seen after a baby has had a convulsion. In most cases, other signs are present to indicate these diagnoses. If any other specific clinical sign is noted in a baby in addition

to lethargy or another non-specific sign, or the baby has two or more non- specific signs, use the chapter Multiple Findings (Most Often Sepsis or Asphyxia) (page F-35).

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has a non-specific sign, such as:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; lethargy;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; drowsiness;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; reduced activity;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; floppiness;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; irritability;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; jitteriness;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; “looks ill.”

(Table F-2, page F-11), and use this information to determine the probable diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother if she received opiate drugs for pain relief during labour

and birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose (page P-13). If the blood glucose is less than

45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l), treat for low blood glucose (page F-91).

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the baby to breastfeed (page C-11). If the baby cannot be

breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-88; Lethargy and other non-specific signs

method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby’s muscle tone and activity at least once daily.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is floppy or lethargic, handle and move the baby gently to

prevent injury when the baby’s muscle tone is low. Support the baby’s entire body, especially the head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use Table F-19 (page F-89) to determine the probable diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a specific problem still is not found:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess the baby for additional signs every two hours for the next six

hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the baby is fed and kept warm;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If a specific problem is noted during the observation period,

follow instructions in the appropriate chapter;

### General Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby seems well at the end of the observation period,

discharge the baby (page C-67), and tell the mother to bring the baby back if a problem is suspected.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Lethargy and other non-specific signs; F-89

## Differential Diagnosis

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-19; Differential diagnosis of non-specific signs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Findingsa; Probable Diagnosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: History; Examination

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Opiate drug given to mother; • Lethargy; Drug-induced

- Dòng dữ liệu: during labour or birth; • Respiratory rate less; lethargy, below

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Time of onset at birth; than 30 breaths per

minute

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Complicated or difficult labour or; • Baby looks ill; Suspected asphyxia

- Dòng dữ liệu: birth (fetal distress); or sepsis, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Failure of baby to spontaneously; F-35

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 2

breathe at birth • Resuscitation at birth • Maternal uterine infection or fever any time from the onset of labour to three days after birth, or rupture of membranes for more than 18 hours before birth a The diagnosis cannot be made if a finding listed in bold is absent. The presence of a finding listed in bold, however, does not guarantee the diagnosis. Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot

be used to rule out the diagnosis.

## Drug-Induced Lethargy

### Drug-Induced Lethargy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide general care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s respiratory rate is less than 30 breaths per minute, give

oxygen (page C-25) at a moderate flow rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is not breathing at all, is gasping, or has a respiratory

rate less than 20 breaths per minute, resuscitate the baby using a bag and mask (page P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is still lethargic after six hours, suspect sepsis or asphyxia

(page F-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-90; Lethargy and other non-specific signs

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s blood glucose is less than 45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l).

## Blood Glucose Less Than 25 Mg/Dl (1.1 Mmol/L)

### Blood Glucose Less Than 25 Mg/Dl (1.1 Mmol/L) — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21) if one is not already in place. Give a

bolus of 2 ml/kg body weight of 10% glucose IV slowly over five minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an IV line cannot be established quickly, give 2 ml/kg body weight

of 10% glucose by gastric tube (page P-33).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse 10% glucose at the daily maintenance volume according to the

baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose 30 minutes after the bolus of glucose and then

every three hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood glucose is less than 25 mg/dl, repeat the bolus of

glucose (above) and continue the infusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood glucose is less than 45 mg/dl but is at least 25 mg/dl

at any measurement, continue the infusion and repeat the blood glucose measurement every three hours until the blood glucose is 45 mg/dl or more on two consecutive measurements;

### Blood Glucose Less Than 25 Mg/Dl (1.1 Mmol/L) — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once the blood glucose is 45 mg/dl or more for two consecutive

measurements, follow instructions for frequency of blood glucose measurements after blood glucose returns to normal (page F-92).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot

be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; As the baby’s ability to feed improves, slowly decrease (over a three-day

period) the volume of IV glucose while increasing the volume of oral feeds. Do not discontinue the glucose infusion abruptly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-92; Low blood glucose

## Blood Glucose Less Than 45 Mg/Dl But At Least 25 Mg/Dl

### Blood Glucose Less Than 45 Mg/Dl But At Least 25 Mg/Dl — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot

be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose in three hours or before the next feed:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood glucose is less than 25 mg/dl, treat as described on

page F-91;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood glucose is still less than 45 mg/dl but at least 25 mg/dl,

increase the frequency of breastfeeding or increase the volume of expressed breast milk given;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once the blood glucose is 45 mg/dl or more on two consecutive

measurements, follow instructions for frequency of blood glucose measurements after blood glucose returns to normal (below).

## Blood Glucose Returns To Normal

### Blood Glucose Returns To Normal — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is receiving IV fluid for any reason, continue blood glucose

measurements every 12 hours for as long as the baby requires IV fluid. If the blood glucose is less than 45 mg/dl, treat as described above.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby no longer requires or is not receiving IV fluid, measure

blood glucose every 12 hours for 24 hours (two more measurements):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood glucose is less than 45 mg/dl, treat as described above;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood glucose remains normal, discontinue measurements.

## Feeding Difficulty F-93

### Feeding Difficulty F-93 — phần 1

Feeding difficulty is common in babies during the first days of life. The difficulty is associated with incorrect breastfeeding technique, small size, or illness. This chapter covers feeding difficulty in the absence of other signs of illness. If the baby has other problems, such as breathing difficulty or abnormal body temperature, in addition to feeding difficulty, see the

## chapter Multiple Findings (Most Often Sepsis or Asphyxia) (page F-35). If

### chapter Multiple Findings (Most Often Sepsis or Asphyxia) (page F-35). If — phần 1

the feeding problem consists primarily of vomiting, see the chapter Vomiting and/or Abdominal Distension (page F-99). Small babies often have difficulty feeding; as they grow, feeding improves. If the baby weighed less than 2.5 kg at birth or was born before 37 weeks gestation, continue with this chapter to evaluate and treat for a specific feeding problem(s). After addressing the problem, see page F-24 for general principles of feeding a small baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby fed well at birth but now is feeding poorly or has stopped

feeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has not fed well since birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby is not gaining weight (proven or suspected).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The mother has not been able to breastfeed successfully.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby is having difficulty feeding and is small or a twin.

### chapter Multiple Findings (Most Often Sepsis or Asphyxia) (page F-35). If — phần 2

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information to determine the probable diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; How do you feed the baby?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; How much did the baby weigh at birth and any time afterwards?

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother to put the baby to her breast. Observe her breastfeeding

for about five minutes, assessing for correct positioning and attachment (page C-12). If the baby is not ready to feed, continue with general management (below), and observe the mother’s technique the next time the baby is ready to feed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-94; Feeding difficulty

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has retained feeds without coughing, choking, or

regurgitating since the first feeding after birth, continue with differential diagnosis (Table F-20, below).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has been coughing, choking, or regurgitating since the

first attempt to feed, try to insert a gastric tube (page P-33):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the gastric tube does not pass or the tip of the tube returns and

the baby is choking and vomiting immediately after swallowing, the baby likely has esophageal atresia or tracheo-esophageal fistula, and urgent surgery is necessary. Organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre for surgery, if possible;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the gastric tube passes, confirm proper placement of the tube in

the stomach (page P-35), aspirate the stomach contents, and continue with Table F-20 to determine the cause of the feeding problem.

## Differential Diagnosis

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-20; Differential diagnosis of feeding difficulty

- Dòng dữ liệu: Findingsa; Probable Diagnosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: History; Examination

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Poor or no feeding after; • Baby looks ill; Suspected sepsis, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Time of onset day 1 or; • Abdominal distension

- Dòng dữ liệu: later; • Breathing difficulty

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Maternal uterine infection; • Abnormal body

- Dòng dữ liệu: or fever any time from the; temperature

- Dòng dữ liệu: onset of labour to three; • Irritability or lethargy

- Dòng dữ liệu: days after birth, or rupture; • Convulsions or

than 18 hours before birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: Feeding difficulty; F-95

- Dòng dữ liệu: History; Examination

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Twin or small baby (less; • Breastfeeding technique; Small baby or twin,

- Dòng dữ liệu: than 2.5 kg at birth or; correct, but baby is not yet; page F-96

- Dòng dữ liệu: born before 37 weeks; ready to breastfeed

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Baby gaining less than; • Baby looks otherwise well; Inadequate weight

day over three days

• Time of onset day 1 or later

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Baby has coughed,; • Gastric tube does not pass Suspected

every feeding • Time of onset at birth a The diagnosis cannot be made if a finding listed in bold is absent. The presence of a finding listed in bold, however, does not guarantee the diagnosis. The diagnosis is definitively confirmed if a finding listed in italics is present. Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot be used to rule out the diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-96; Feeding difficulty

## Small Baby Or Twin

### Small Baby Or Twin — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is small, see page F-24 for general principles of feeding a

small baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is a twin, see page C-14 for information on feeding twins.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in two days to reassess feeding and weight gain.

## Incorrect Positioning And Attachment

### Incorrect Positioning And Attachment — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note previously recorded weights, if available, and weigh the baby daily.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Help the mother improve her breastfeeding technique:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother’s breastfeeding technique is correct, reassure the

mother, and continue to observe her technique during a three-day period;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother’s breastfeeding technique is incorrect, help the

mother to achieve the correct technique (page C-12);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has gained at least 15 g/kg body weight per day over

three days, reassure the mother that her milk supply is adequate and explain appropriate feeding practices to her. If there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has not gained at least 15 g/kg body weight per day

over three days, treat for inadequate weight gain (below).

## Inadequate Weight Gain

### Inadequate Weight Gain — phần 1

Note that the management described below for inadequate weight gain applies only to babies on full oral feeds via any method (breastfeeding or receiving expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method). If the baby is receiving IV fluid and getting less than full (or no) oral feeds and daily weight loss is more than 5%, increase the total volume of fluid by 10 ml/kg body weight for one day to compensate for inadequate fluid administration.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note previously recorded weights and weigh the baby daily.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Feeding difficulty; F-97

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm poor weight gain if the baby has gained less than 15 g/kg body

weight per day over the last three days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check for and correct or treat obvious causes of inadequate weight gain:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Determine if the baby is being fed frequently enough (i.e. eight times

### Inadequate Weight Gain — phần 2

in 24 hours), particularly at night. If the baby is being fed using an alternative feeding method, ensure that the baby is receiving the correct volume of feeds (Table C-4, page C-22);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Determine if the ambient temperature is optimal; if the ambient

temperature is too cold or too hot, the baby will utilize more energy for temperature regulation and less for growth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Look for signs of sepsis (e.g. poor feeding, vomiting, breathing

difficulty; Table F-9, page F-37);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Look for thrush in the baby’s mouth (Table F-25, page F-128).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If poor weight gain is confirmed and there are no obvious causes or

### Inadequate Weight Gain — phần 3

if an obvious cause was found and treated (e.g. the temperature has been corrected for three days or thrush or sepsis has been treated for seven days) and weight gain is still inadequate, work with the mother for three days to increase the quantity of breast milk that the baby receives.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the average measured weight gain after three days is at least

15 g/kg body weight per day:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain appropriate feeding practices to the mother and what to

expect regarding her baby’s growth (page C-53);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge

the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Obtain follow-up weights weekly for one month to verify progress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the average measured weight gain after three days is less than

15 g/kg body weight per day despite the measures above:

### Inadequate Weight Gain — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have the mother supplement breastfeeding by expressing breast milk

between feedings and giving this to the baby as a supplement, using an alternative feeding method (page C-14), after the baby has breastfed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother cannot express breast milk, give the baby 10 ml of

breast-milk substitute (page C-19) by cup/spoon (page C-16) after

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-98; Feeding difficulty

each time at the breast. Breast-milk substitute should not be used unless it can be ensured that the substitute is:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; available for the entire period needed (this might include some

time after discharge);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; affordable for the health care facility as well as the family;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; used safely;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; prepared in a sterile manner according to instructions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Continue supplementation with expressed breast milk or breast-milk

### Inadequate Weight Gain — phần 5

substitute until the baby’s weight gain is at least 15 g/kg body weight per day for three consecutive days, and then decrease the supplementary feedings to 5 ml per feed for two days:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If weight gain continues to be adequate (15 g/kg body weight

per day or more) for two more days, discontinue supplements completely;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If weight gain becomes inadequate again (less than 15 g/kg

body weight per day), begin supplementing again with 10 ml of expressed breast milk or breast-milk substitute per feed, and repeat the process.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s weight gain for three more days. If weight gain

continues at the same or better rate, the baby is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Vomiting And/Or Abdominal Distension F-99

### Vomiting And/Or Abdominal Distension F-99 — phần 1

Spitting or regurgitation of small quantities of milk after a feed is common in a newborn baby, is usually transitory, and has no effect on growth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A baby is vomiting:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; forcefully;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; regardless of method of feeding;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; entire feedings after every feed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; bile or blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A baby has abdominal distension (Fig. F-6).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-6; Abdominal distension

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information to determine the probable diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother (or whoever brought the baby in):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Did the vomiting begin at the first feed or did it start later?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; How long after a feeding does the baby vomit?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; What does the vomitus look like? Is it frothy, or does it contain bile

or blood?

### Vomiting And/Or Abdominal Distension F-99 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Has the baby passed meconium? Was meconium in the amniotic

fluid?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Are your nipples cracked or sore?

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-100; Vomiting and/or abdominal distension

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Look for:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; abdominal tenderness (baby cries when abdomen is gently pressed);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; imperforate anus (if the baby has an imperforate anus, see page

F-153 for management).

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a gastric tube via the nasal route (page P-33):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the gastric tube does not pass or the tip of the tube returns and

the baby is choking and vomiting immediately after swallowing, the baby likely has esophageal atresia or tracheo-esophageal fistula, and urgent surgery is necessary. Organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre for surgery, if possible;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the gastric tube passes, confirm that the tube is properly

positioned in the stomach (page P-35), and aspirate the stomach contents.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby appears to be seriously ill (e.g. floppy and lethargic) or

small (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation), establish an IV line (page P-21), and give IV fluid at maintenance volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22).

### General Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use Table F-21 (page F-101) to determine the probable cause of

vomiting and/or abdominal distension. If the cause of vomiting cannot be determined, see page F-103.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vomiting and/or abdominal distension; F-101

## Differential Diagnosis

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-21; Differential diagnosis of vomiting and/or abdominal

more than 18 hours before birth • Poor or no feeding after having fed well

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Poor or no feeding; • Floppiness or; • Sepsis; Necrotizing

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2 to 10; • Abdominal; gastric aspirates

distension, tenderness • Small baby (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation) • Blood or bile in vomitus • Blood or mucus in stool • Diarrhoea • Pallor • Progressive signs of ill health (temperature instability and/or apnoea)

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-102; Vomiting and/or abdominal distension

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-21 Cont.; Differential diagnosis of vomiting and/or abdominal

Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot be used to rule out the diagnosis.

## Cause Of Vomiting Not Determined

### Cause Of Vomiting Not Determined — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22) for the first 12 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 12 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has no other signs besides vomiting after the 12-hour

period:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18) for 24 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If these feeds are retained, allow the baby to begin breastfeeding

(page C-11), or give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Remove the gastric tube after two successful feeds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If vomiting continues or any other signs are present (e.g. blood in

vomitus, forceful vomiting, abdominal distension), try again to determine the cause of vomiting (Table F-21, page F-101).

### Cause Of Vomiting Not Determined — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cause of vomiting still cannot be determined, treat for sepsis

(page F-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-104; Vomiting and/or abdominal distension

## Necrotizing Enterocolitis

### Necrotizing Enterocolitis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21) if one is not already in place, and give

only IV fluid at maintenance volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22) for the first five days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41), and ensure that the baby is not fed for the

first five days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a gastric tube (page P-33) and ensure free drainage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an abdominal mass becomes palpable, it is likely that the baby has a

bowel perforation or intestinal obstruction from an abscess. Organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre for surgery, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure haemoglobin daily until bleeding stops, and then measure once

more after 24 hours. If the haemoglobin is less than 8 g/dl (haematocrit less than 24%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

### Necrotizing Enterocolitis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the baby’s heart and respiratory rates every three hours for three

days after the last blood transfusion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for five days. If, after the five-day period, abdominal

distension has decreased, there is minimal gastric aspirate, and the baby is passing non-bloody stool:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give the baby expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Begin feeding the volume of milk required on day 1, regardless of

the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once adequate volumes of milk are tolerated for 48 hours without

vomiting, allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics:

### Necrotizing Enterocolitis — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s heart and respiratory rates are stable, the baby has not

required a transfusion for at least 48 hours and is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vomiting and/or abdominal distension; F-105

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure haemoglobin weekly for one month. If the haemoglobin is

less than 8 g/dl (haematocrit less than 24%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; To prevent iron deficiency anaemia, give small babies an oral iron

preparation to give elemental iron 2 mg/kg body weight once daily from two months of age up to 23 months of age.

## Obstruction

### Obstruction — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a gastric tube was inserted, keep it in place and ensure free drainage

(page P-36).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer the baby to a tertiary

hospital or specialized centre for surgery, if possible.

## Swallowed Maternal Blood

### Swallowed Maternal Blood — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If vomiting is caused by blood swallowed during breastfeeding

(cracked nipples):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Observe the mother while she breastfeeds. Assess her technique

(page C-12) and suggest changes as necessary;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If breastfeeding is painful for the mother, encourage the mother to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Express some breast milk (page C-15) to stimulate the milk let-

down reflex before putting the baby to her breast;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Begin to nurse on the side that is less sore until the let-down

occurs, then switch the baby to the affected breast;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Express a little milk or colostrum onto her nipples after

breastfeeding;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Expose her nipples to the air to prevent maceration and promote

healing;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If cracking is severe on only one nipple, have the mother

### Swallowed Maternal Blood — phần 2

breastfeed on the other side exclusively for two or more days while the affected nipple heals. During this time she can express

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-106; Vomiting and/or abdominal distension

the breast milk of the affected breast and feed this to the baby using an alternative feeding method (page C-14);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If vomiting is caused by blood swallowed at the time of birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Attach a syringe to the gastric tube and aspirate the stomach

contents;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby

cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Remove the gastric tube after two successful feeds;

### Swallowed Maternal Blood — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Gastric Irritation

### Gastric Irritation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot

be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has had two successful breastfeeds, or is feeding well using

an alternative feeding method, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby continues to regurgitate all feeds after 24 hours, the

problem is probably due to an abnormality of the gastrointestinal tract:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer the baby to a

tertiary hospital or specialized centre for further evaluation, if possible.

## Diarrhoea F-107

### Diarrhoea F-107 — phần 1

There are non-infectious causes of diarrhoea, but sepsis is the most common cause during the newborn period.

Observe strict infection prevention practices (page C-37) at all times when caring for any baby with diarrhoea to prevent spreading one baby’s infection to other babies in the newborn special care unit. Wear gloves when handling soiled napkins and other items used to care for the baby, and carefully wash hands (page C-38) after handling a baby with diarrhoea.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby is passing stool with increased frequency.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s stool is watery or green, or contains mucus or blood.

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information to determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-22, page F-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother (or whoever brought the baby in):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Is the baby being fed any foods or fluid other than breast milk?

### Diarrhoea F-107 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; What does the baby’s stool look like? Is it watery or green, or does it

contain mucus or blood?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; How frequently has the baby been passing stool?

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Look for:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; signs of dehydration (e.g. sunken eyes or fontanelle, loss of skin

elasticity, or dry tongue and mucous membranes);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; signs of sepsis (e.g. poor feeding, vomiting, breathing difficulty;

Table F-9, page F-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-108; Diarrhoea

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11). If the baby cannot

be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is giving the baby any food or fluid other than breast

milk, advise her to stop giving them.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oral rehydration solution (ORS) for every diarrhoeal stool passed:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is able to feed, have the mother breastfeed more often,

or give ORS 20 ml/kg body weight between breastfeeds using a cup, cup and spoon, or other device (page C-16);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is not feeding well, insert a gastric tube (page P-33),

and give ORS 20 ml/kg body weight by tube;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If prepackaged ORS is not available, make ORS as follows:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use recently boiled and cooled water;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; To 1 litre of water, add:

### General Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; sodium chloride 3.5 g;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; trisodium citrate 2.9 g (or sodium bicarbonate 2.5 g);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; potassium chloride 1.5 g;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; glucose (anhydrous) 20 g (or sucrose [common sugar] 40 g).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has signs of dehydration or sepsis, establish an IV line

(page P-21), and give IV fluid while allowing the baby to continue to breastfeed:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of dehydration, increase the volume of fluid by

10% of the baby’s body weight on the first day that the dehydration is noted;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby receives a sufficient volume of fluid to meet rehydration

and maintenance requirements and to replace ongoing losses, the use of ORS is not necessary;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess the baby again in 12 hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is still having diarrhoeal stools, continue the

### General Management — phần 3

increased volume of IV fluid for an additional 24 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Diarrhoea; F-109

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has not had a diarrhoeal stool in the last 12

hours, adjust fluid to maintenance volume according to the baby's age (Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-22).

## Management Of Nosocomial Diarrhoea

### Management Of Nosocomial Diarrhoea — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the diarrhoea developed while the baby was hospitalized and more

than one baby with diarrhoea from the same ward is seen within a two-day period, suspect a nosocomial infection (page C-45).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Isolate the baby from other babies, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to provide general management for diarrhoea (page F-108).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-112; Diarrhoea

## Bleeding And/Or Pallor F-113

### Bleeding And/Or Pallor F-113 — phần 1

This chapter covers the baby who is bleeding or is found to be pale, either at birth or any time thereafter, with or without signs of internal or external bleeding. Pallor can be a sign of anaemia, shock, or both.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby is currently bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has a history of bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby appears pale at birth or sometime thereafter.

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information to determine the probable diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother (or whoever brought the baby in):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Is there blood in the baby’s stool or urine?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Did you have a previous baby with haemolytic jaundice, glucose-6-

phosphate dehydrogenase (G6PD) deficiency, or Rhesus (Rh) factor or ABO blood group incompatibility?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Was there any abnormal bleeding during pregnancy or labour/birth?

### Bleeding And/Or Pallor F-113 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Was the baby’s umbilical cord clamped/tied correctly at birth?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Is the baby vomiting? If so, is there blood or bile in the vomitus?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Is the baby a twin? If so, is the other twin very pink or red (i.e. twin-

to-twin transfusion)?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have multiple blood samples been taken from the baby?

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Look for:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; where the blood is coming from (e.g. umbilicus, male circumcision

site, or venepuncture site);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; generalized oedema (body swelling);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; abdominal tenderness (baby cries when abdomen is gently pressed);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; jaundice.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-114; Bleeding and/or pallor

## Bleeding

### Bleeding — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide immediate management for bleeding as described in Table F-1

(page F-6), if not already done.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is still bleeding, increase the rate of infusion of IV fluid to

infuse 20 ml/kg body weight of fluid over the first hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of shock (e.g. pallor, cold to the touch, heart rate more

than 180 beats per minute, unconscious or nearly unconscious) or if signs of shock develop while the baby is being assessed:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Infuse normal saline or Ringer’s lactate 10 ml/kg body weight over

10 minutes, and repeat once after 20 minutes if signs of shock continue;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give a blood transfusion (page P-31) immediately using type O,

Rh-negative blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9), and measure haemoglobin. If the

haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

### Bleeding — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-23, page F-115).

## Pallor Without A History Of Bleeding

### Pallor Without A History Of Bleeding — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of shock (e.g. cold to the touch, heart rate more than

180 beats per minute, unconscious or nearly unconscious) or if signs of shock develop while the baby is being assessed, establish an IV line (page P-21), if one is not already in place, and infuse normal saline or Ringer’s lactate 10 ml/kg body weight over 10 minutes, and repeat once after 20 minutes if signs of shock continue.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure blood glucose. If the blood glucose is less than 45 mg/dl

(2.6 mmol/l), treat for low blood glucose (page F-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9), and measure haemoglobin. If the

haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-23, page F-115).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Bleeding and/or pallor; F-115

a The diagnosis cannot be made if a finding listed in bold is absent. The presence of a finding listed in bold, however, does not guarantee the diagnosis. The diagnosis is definitively confirmed if a finding listed in italics is present. Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot be used to rule out the diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Bleeding and/or pallor; F-119

## Haemorrhagic Disease Of The Newborn Baby

### Haemorrhagic Disease Of The Newborn Baby — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding does not stop within three hours, treat for sepsis (page F-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9) and measure haemoglobin once daily. If

the haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide ongoing management (page F-120).

## Coagulopathy

### Coagulopathy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9) and measure haemoglobin once daily. If

the haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide ongoing management (page F-120).

## Possible Blood Loss From Obstetric Causes

### Possible Blood Loss From Obstetric Causes — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9) and measure haemoglobin once daily:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%),

give a blood transfusion (page P-31);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the haemoglobin is between 10 and 13 g/dl (haematocrit

between 30 and 40%) and there are signs of shock (e.g. pallor, cold to the touch, heart rate more than 180 beats per minute, unconscious or nearly unconscious), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide ongoing management (page F-120).

## Unknown Origin

### Unknown Origin — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby’s condition is stable, measure haemoglobin weekly for as

long as the baby remains in the hospital. If the haemoglobin is less than 8 g/dl (haematocrit less than 24%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide ongoing management (below).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-120; Bleeding and/or pallor

## Ongoing Management Of Babies With Pallor Or Bleeding

### Ongoing Management Of Babies With Pallor Or Bleeding — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discontinue IV fluid unless an IV line is needed for another reason. If IV

fluid is still required, continue IV fluid and ensure that the total fluid volume the first day (from both oral and IV sources) equals daily maintenance volume according to the baby’s age plus an additional 10% of the total fluid volume required for the day (Table C-4, page C-22). Use the maintenance fluid volume for subsequent days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure haemoglobin daily until the haemoglobin is stable for three days

and at a level not requiring transfusion, and then weekly for as long as the baby is in the hospital.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the heart and respiratory rates every three hours until the baby’s

condition is stable.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s heart and respiratory rates are stable, the baby has not required

### Ongoing Management Of Babies With Pallor Or Bleeding — phần 2

a transfusion for at least 48 hours, is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To prevent iron deficiency anaemia, give small babies an oral iron

preparation to give elemental iron 2 mg/kg body weight once daily from two months of age up to 23 months of age.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up twice weekly for two weeks after discharge to monitor feeding

and growth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure haemoglobin again in one month. If the haemoglobin is less

than 8 g/dl (haematocrit less than 24%), give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

## Swelling On Scalp F-121

### Swelling On Scalp F-121 — phần 1

Most swellings on the baby’s scalp originating at birth are minor and resolve spontaneously; however, subaponeurotic (subgaleal) bleeding can be life- threatening and must be recognized and treated immediately.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has a swelling on the scalp at birth or soon thereafter.

(Table F-2, page F-11), looking especially for findings of pallor or increased heart or respiratory or rates (consistently more than 160 beats per minute or 60 breaths per minute, respectively).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain the following additional information to determine the probable

diagnosis (Table F-24, page F-122).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine (feel):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the location and boundary of the scalp swelling;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if the swelling is fluctuant (feeling of free liquid) or if there is an

area within the swelling that feels spongy;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if the scalp is painful (baby cries when scalp is touched);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if there is moulding of the head (Fig. F-7) with over-riding of the

parietal bones.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-7; Moulding of the baby’s head

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-122; Swelling on scalp

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Asphyxia; more than 60 breaths per

minute • Increasing head circumference

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Swelling apparent; • Swelling over presenting part; Caput succedaneum,

- Dòng dữ liệu: at birth; of head that is firm and not; page F-124

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 2

fluctuant • Moulding of head and over- riding of parietal bones • Baby looks otherwise well

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Swelling apparent; • Rounded swelling with; Cephalohaematoma,
