Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot be used to rule out the diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Swelling on scalp; F-123

## Subaponeurotic (Subgaleal) Haemorrhage

### Subaponeurotic (Subgaleal) Haemorrhage — phần 1

Babies with subaponeurotic haemorrhage may have a range of problems. Examine the baby closely for other signs, including increasing head circumference, episodes of apnoea, or rapid heart or respiratory rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give vitamin K1 (phytomenadione) 1 mg IM once (or IV if an IV line has

already been established), even if the baby received it at birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure haemoglobin now and again in 24 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the haemoglobin is less than 10 g/dl (haematocrit less than 30%)

give a blood transfusion (page P-31).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for pallor and check the heart and respiratory rates

every hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure the baby’s head circumference every six hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s head circumference is increasing or there are signs of

### Subaponeurotic (Subgaleal) Haemorrhage — phần 2

shock (pallor, cold to the touch, heart rate more than 180 beats per minute, unconscious or nearly unconscious), transfuse immediately with type O, Rh-negative blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby is fed:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Allow the baby to begin breastfeeding (page C-11);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breast milk using

an alternative feeding method (page C-14);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby cannot take enough milk by breastfeeding or using

an alternative feeding method, establish an IV line (page P-21) and give IV fluid;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the baby receives feeds and fluid at maintenance volume

according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the baby is stable:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure head circumference daily;

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-124; Swelling on scalp

### Subaponeurotic (Subgaleal) Haemorrhage — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check pallor and the heart and respiratory rates every three hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Be prepared at any time to give fluid as indicated above;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the haemoglobin is less than 8 g/dl (haematocrit less than 24%),

give a blood transfusion (page P-31);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the haemoglobin is more than 10 g/dl (haematocrit more than

30%), measure haemoglobin two more times after three and six days, and then weekly for as long as the baby is in the hospital.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe for jaundice (page F-77) once daily, and give phototherapy if

necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the head circumference begins to decrease:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Continue observation until the baby is at least four days old and the

haemoglobin has remained at a level not requiring transfusion for at least three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; After the observation period, if the baby’s heart and respiratory rates

### Subaponeurotic (Subgaleal) Haemorrhage — phần 4

are stable, the baby is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Caput Succedaneum, Chignon, Or Cephalohaematoma

### Caput Succedaneum, Chignon, Or Cephalohaematoma — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassure the mother that these are not serious conditions and that they do

not require treatment. Caput and chignon resolve spontaneously after two to three days. Cephalohaematoma (Fig. F-8, page F-125) resolves over several weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not aspirate a cephalohaematoma, even though it feels fluctuant.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the mother to bring the baby back if the baby develops jaundice

(page F-77).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Swelling on scalp; F-125

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-8; Baby with unilateral cephalohaematoma

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-126; Swelling on scalp

## Skin And Mucous Membrane Problems F-127

### Skin And Mucous Membrane Problems F-127 — phần 1

Skin infections in babies are extremely contagious. Observe strict infection prevention practices (page C-37) at all times to prevent spreading one baby’s infection to other babies in the nursery. Dispose of all items in direct contact with the lesions (e.g. gauze) in a plastic bag or leakproof, covered waste container.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s skin or soft tissues are red or swollen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has pustules or blisters on the skin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has white patches on the tongue or inside the mouth.

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information to determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-25, page F-128).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother (or whoever brought the baby in):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; What did the lesions look like and where on the baby’s body were

they when they first appeared?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have the lesions changed at all since they first appeared?

### Skin And Mucous Membrane Problems F-127 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Look for:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; location of lesions (e.g. in the axillae, around the umbilicus and

groin, on the palms and soles);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; characteristics of lesions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; pustules (less than 1 cm in diameter) or blisters (1 cm in

diameter or more);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; reddened skin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; blistering skin rash.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; swelling on skin that is tender (baby cries when swelling is touched)

or fluctuant (feeling of free liquid in swollen area).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has a cut or abrasion, see page F-132.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-128; Skin and mucous membrane problems

## Differential Diagnosis

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-25; Differential diagnosis of skin and mucous membrane

problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: Findingsa; Probable Diagnosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: History; Examination

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Time of onset day 1 or; • Pustules or blisters; Skin infection, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: later; • Lesions prevalent on back of; F-129

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Lesions solitary at first,; hands, around neck, in axillae,

- Dòng dữ liệu: then developing into; and around umbilicus and groin

clusters and spreading to other areas

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Time of onset day 3 or; • Red skin and swollen; Cellulitis/abscess,

- Dòng dữ liệu: later; subcutaneous tissue anywhere; page F-130

on body • Tender and/or fluctuant swelling

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Time of onset day 3 or; • Bright red patches on skin in; Thrush in napkin

- Dòng dữ liệu: later; napkin area, often scaly in; area, page F-131

appearance or with small white centres

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Time of onset day 3 or; • Thick white patches on tongue; Thrush in mouth,

- Dòng dữ liệu: later; or inside mouth; page F-131

- Dòng dữ liệu: • If mother had a; • Generalized oedema (body; Congenital syphilis,

- Dòng dữ liệu: serologic test for; swelling); page F-46

- Dòng dữ liệu: syphilis during; • Abdominal distension (from

- Dòng dữ liệu: pregnancy, it was; enlarged liver and/or spleen or

- Dòng dữ liệu: positive; from fluid in abdomen)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Mother not treated or; • Blistering skin rash on palms and

- Dòng dữ liệu: treated inadequately; soles

- Dòng dữ liệu: for syphilis; • Profuse nasal discharge

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Time of onset at birth; (“snuffles”)

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 3

a The diagnosis cannot be made if a finding listed in bold is absent. The presence of a finding listed in bold, however, does not guarantee the diagnosis. The diagnosis is definitively confirmed if a finding listed in italics is present. Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot be used to rule out the diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Skin and mucous membrane problems; F-129

## Skin Infection

### Skin Infection — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the infection developed while the baby was hospitalized or more

than one baby with a skin infection from the same ward is seen within a two-day period, suspect a nosocomial infection (page C-45).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing clean examination gloves:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wash the affected area(s) of skin using an antiseptic solution (Table

C-10, page C-41) and clean gauze sponges;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Swab the pustules/blisters with 0.5% gentian violet solution;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Repeat four times daily until the pustules/blisters are gone. Have the

mother do this whenever possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe for signs of sepsis (e.g. poor feeding, vomiting, breathing

difficulty; Table F-9, page F-37), and treat for sepsis (page F-41) if found.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Count the number of pustules or blisters, determine whether they cover

less or more than half of the body, and treat as described below.

## Body With No Signs Of Sepsis

### Body With No Signs Of Sepsis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for five days:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the pustules/blisters clear within five days and there are no other

problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If most of the pustules/blisters are still present after five days but

the baby does not have signs of sepsis, give cloxacillin by mouth according to the baby’s age and weight (Table C-9, page C-35) for five days.

## Body With No Signs Of Sepsis

### Body With No Signs Of Sepsis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the white centre of a pustule using a sterile lancet. Take a specimen

of pus using a sterile cotton swab, and send it to the laboratory for culture and sensitivity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give cloxacillin IM according to the baby’s age and weight (Table C-9,

page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-130; Skin and mucous membrane problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby’s condition at least once daily for signs of improvement

(pustules/blisters are not spreading and are beginning to dry up and heal):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the pustules/blisters are improving after three days of

treatment with antibiotics, continue cloxacillin to complete five days of treatment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the pustules/blisters are not improving after three days of

treatment with antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the culture is positive, change the antibiotic according to the

### Body With No Signs Of Sepsis — phần 2

results of the culture and sensitivity, and give the antibiotic for an additional five days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the culture is not possible or the organism cannot be

identified, continue giving cloxacillin and also give gentamicin according to the baby’s age and weight (Table C-9, page C-35) for seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics. If the

pustules/blisters have cleared, the baby is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Skin Pustules/Blisters With Signs Of Sepsis

### Skin Pustules/Blisters With Signs Of Sepsis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41), but give cloxacillin IV according to the

baby’s age and weight (Table C-9, page C-35) instead of ampicillin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is already receiving antibiotics for sepsis, discontinue

ampicillin. Give cloxacillin IV according to the baby’s age and weight (Table C-9, page C-35) in addition to gentamicin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics. If the

pustules/blisters have cleared, the baby is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Cellulitis/Abscess

### Cellulitis/Abscess — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is a fluctuant swelling, incise and drain the abscess (page P-43).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give cloxacillin IM according to the baby’s age and weight (Table C-9,

page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Skin and mucous membrane problems; F-131

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby’s condition at least once daily for signs of improvement:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cellulitis/abscess is improving after five days of treatment

with the antibiotic, continue cloxacillin to complete 10 days of treatment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cellulitis/abscess is not improving after five days of

treatment with the antibiotic:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the culture is positive, change the antibiotic according to the

results of the culture and sensitivity and give the antibiotic for an additional 10 days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the culture is not possible or the organism cannot be

### Cellulitis/Abscess — phần 2

identified, continue giving cloxacillin and also give gentamicin according to the baby’s age and weight (Table C-9, page C-35) for 10 days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics. If the

cellulitis/abscess has cleared, the baby is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Thrush In Napkin Area

### Thrush In Napkin Area — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply nystatin cream to the lesions or swab the lesions with 0.5%

gentian violet solution at every napkin change, continuing for three days after the lesions have healed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the napkin is changed whenever it is wet or soiled.

## Thrush In Mouth

### Thrush In Mouth — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Swab the thrush patches in the baby’s mouth with nystatin oral solution

or 0.5% gentian violet solution four times daily, continuing for two days after the lesions have healed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother put nystatin cream or 0.5% gentian violet solution on

her breasts after breastfeeding for as long as the baby is being treated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-132; Skin and mucous membrane problems

CUT

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clean the cut using gauze soaked in antiseptic solution (e.g. 2.5%

polyvidone iodine [Table C-10, page C-41]; note that other antiseptic solutions may burn).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cover the cut with a simple bandage to keep the cut clean and dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the edges of the cut are open, pull them closed with a butterfly

bandage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the

baby (page C-67).

### Thrush In Mouth — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the mother what the signs of local infection are (e.g. redness,

heat, and swelling of the skin around the cut):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ask the mother to bring the baby back if she sees signs of local

infection;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If signs of local infection are seen, remove the bandage and treat

with a topical antibiotic ointment three times daily for five days, leaving the cut uncovered.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother return with the baby in one week to remove the

bandage, if necessary. If there is no infection, no further follow-up is needed.

## Abrasion

### Abrasion — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clean the abrasion (e.g. Fig. F-9) using cotton-wool balls soaked in

antiseptic solution (e.g. 2.5% polyvidone iodine [Table C-10, page C-41]; note that other antiseptic solutions may burn).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-9; Baby with abrasions from forceps delivery

- Dòng dữ liệu: Skin and mucous membrane problems; F-133

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the wound clean and dry, and instruct the mother how to do so.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the

baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the mother what the signs of local infection are (e.g. redness,

heat, and swelling of the skin around the cut):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ask the mother to bring the baby back if she sees signs of local

infection;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If signs of local infection are seen, treat with a topical antibiotic

ointment three times daily for five days, leaving the abrasion uncovered.

### Abrasion — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother return with the baby in one week. If there is no

infection, no further follow-up is needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-134; Skin and mucous membrane problems

## Pus, Or Foul Smelling

### Pus, Or Foul Smelling — phần 1

The umbilicus usually separates one week after birth, and the wound heals within 15 days. Until the wound is healed, this is an important entry point for infection, which can quickly lead to sepsis; early recognition and treatment of an infected umbilicus are essential to prevent sepsis.

Observe strict infection prevention practices (page C-37) at all times to prevent spreading one baby’s infection to other babies in the nursery. Dispose of all items in direct contact with the umbilicus or draining pus in a plastic bag or leakproof, covered waste container.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s umbilicus is swollen, draining pus, or foul smelling

(infected).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The skin around the umbilicus is red and hardened.

(Table F-2, page F-11), and obtain the following additional information to classify the severity of the infection (Table F-26, page F-136).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother (or whoever brought the baby in):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Were unclean or harmful substances (e.g. animal dung) applied to

the baby’s umbilicus?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Was the umbilicus covered (e.g. with a bandage)?

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is redness and swelling of the skin around the umbilicus,

determine how far it extends beyond the umbilicus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-136; Umbilicus red and swollen, draining pus, or foul smelling

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-26; Classification of severity of infection of umbilicus

- Dòng dữ liệu: Findingsa; Classification

- Dòng dữ liệu: History; Examination

### Problem — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Unclean birth; • Umbilicus red and swollen; Severe infection of

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Application of unclean; • Redness and swelling of skin; umbilicus, below

- Dòng dữ liệu: or harmful substances; extending more than 1 cm

- Dòng dữ liệu: (e.g. animal dung) to; beyond umbilicus

- Dòng dữ liệu: umbilicus; • Umbilicus draining pus

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Umbilicus covered; • Foul-smelling umbilicus

• Skin around umbilicus is red and hardened • Abdominal distension

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Umbilicus red and swollen; Local infection of

• Redness and swelling of skin umbilicus, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: extending less than 1 cm; F-137

### Problem — phần 3

beyond umbilicus a The classification cannot be made if a finding listed in bold is absent. The presence of a finding listed in bold, however, does not guarantee the classification. Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the classification, but their absence cannot be used to rule out the classification.

## Severe Infection Of Umbilicus

### Severe Infection Of Umbilicus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the infection developed while the baby was hospitalized or more

than one baby with an infection of the umbilicus from the same ward is seen within a two-day period, suspect a nosocomial infection (page C-45).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat for sepsis (page F-41) but give cloxacillin IV according to the

baby’s age and weight (Table C-9, page C-35) instead of ampicillin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is already receiving antibiotics for sepsis, discontinue

ampicillin. Give cloxacillin IV according to the baby’s age and weight (Table C-9, page C-35) in addition to gentamicin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If skin pustules/blisters are present, treat for a skin infection (page

F-129).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide general care as described for a local infection of the umbilicus

(page F-137).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Umbilicus red and swollen, draining pus, or foul smelling; F-137

### Severe Infection Of Umbilicus — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the baby for 24 hours after discontinuing antibiotics. If the

infection has cleared, the baby is feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Local Infection Of Umbilicus

### Local Infection Of Umbilicus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing clean examination gloves:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wash the umbilicus using an antiseptic solution (Table C-10, page

C-41) and clean gauze sponges;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Swab the umbilicus and the area around it with 0.5% gentian violet

four times daily until there is no more pus coming from the umbilicus. Have the mother do this whenever possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the area of redness and swelling extends more than 1 cm beyond

the umbilicus, treat for severe infection of the umbilicus (page F-136).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the infection has cleared, the baby is feeding well, and there are no other

problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-138; Umbilicus red and swollen, draining pus, or foul smelling

## Eyes Red, Swollen, Or Draining Pus F-139

### Eyes Red, Swollen, Or Draining Pus F-139 — phần 1

Red and swollen eyes or eyes draining pus may be caused by bacteria (e.g. gonococcus, chlamydia, staphylococcus) that are usually transmitted to the baby at the time of birth, or the problem may be caused by chemicals (e.g. silver nitrate eye drops) placed in the baby’s eyes just after birth. Most causes of newborn eye problems will respond to local treatment, but gonococcal and chlamydial infections need to be identified, as they require systemic antibiotics.

Observe strict infection prevention practices (page C-37) at all times to prevent spreading one baby’s infection to other babies in the nursery. Dispose of all items in direct contact with draining pus in a plastic bag or leakproof, covered waste container.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s eye(s) is red, swollen, or draining pus.

(Table F-2, page F-11), looking especially for a history of a sexually transmitted infection in the mother. Obtain the following additional information to determine the probable diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother (or whoever brought the baby in):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Were drops of silver nitrate put in the baby’s eye(s) at birth?

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Was any other prophylactic treatment put in the baby’s eye(s)? If so,

when?

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing clean examination gloves:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Clean the eyelids using sterile normal saline or clean (boiled and

cooled) water and a clean swab, cleaning from the inside edge of the eye to the outside edge;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have the mother do this whenever possible;

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-140; Eyes red, swollen, or draining pus

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Repeat four times daily until the eye problems have cleared.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother wash the baby’s face once daily (or more often, if

necessary) using clean water, and dry with a clean cloth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the problem developed while the baby was hospitalized or more

than one baby with eye problems from the same ward is seen within a two-day period, suspect a nosocomial infection (page C-45).

## Eyes Draining Pus (Conjunctivitis)

### Eyes Draining Pus (Conjunctivitis) — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a specimen of pus, if it can be easily obtained, using a sterile cotton

swab (take care to avoid direct contact with the baby’s eyes):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Smear the pus on a slide, stain the slide with Gram stain, and view

the slide under a microscope;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Send a sample of the pus to the laboratory for culture (including

possible gonococcus) and sensitivity;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-27, page F-141).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If it is not possible to perform a Gram stain or culture and

sensitivity, see page F-143.

## Draining From Eyes

### Draining From Eyes — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the eye problem continues for more than four days (despite the

general management described above), but there is still no pus draining from the eyes:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give erythromycin by mouth (Table C-9, page C-35) for 14 days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Apply 1% tetracycline ointment to the affected eye(s) four times

daily until the eye(s) is no longer red, swollen, or sticky.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If pus begins to drain from the eye, see above.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the

baby (page C-67), and have the mother continue the treatment at home.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Eyes red, swollen, or draining pus; F-141

## Differential Diagnosis

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-27; Differential diagnosis of conjunctivitis

Investigation and Culture Results

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3 or later; • Moderate amount; cocci in clusters; due to

- Dòng dữ liệu: for staphylococcus; aureus, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: sexually; involved; diplococci; due to

- Dòng dữ liệu: infection; pus; for gonococcus; page F-142

• Eye prophylaxis either not given or given after first hour of life • Time of onset day 1 or later

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Watery discharge; • Both eyes; • No organisms; Conjunctivitis

- Dòng dữ liệu: from eyes at first,; involved; seen on Gram; due to

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Silver nitrate; • Both eyes; • No organisms; Chemical

- Dòng dữ liệu: at birth; • Eyes red and; stain; No treatment is

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1 or 2; • Small amount of

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 3

pus a The diagnosis cannot be made if a finding listed in bold is absent. The presence of a finding listed in bold, however, does not guarantee the diagnosis. The diagnosis is definitively confirmed if a finding listed in italics is present. Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot be used to rule out the diagnosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-142; Eyes red, swollen, or draining pus

## Conjunctivitis Due To S. Aureus

### Conjunctivitis Due To S. Aureus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply 1% tetracycline ointment to the affected eye(s) four times daily for

five days. There is no need for systemic antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to clean the baby’s eyes and wash the baby’s face as described

under general management (page F-139).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother and baby can stay near the health care facility, the baby

does not have to be admitted to the hospital for this treatment.

## Conjunctivitis Due To Gonorrhoea

### Conjunctivitis Due To Gonorrhoea — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give ceftriaxone IM (Table C-9, page C-34) in a single dose.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There is no need for antibiotic eye ointment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to clean the baby’s eyes and wash the baby’s face as described

under general management (page F-139).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother and baby can stay near the health care facility, the baby

does not have to be admitted to the hospital for this treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat the mother and her partner(s) for gonorrhoea if not already treated

(modify treatment according to local susceptibility, if necessary). Give:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ceftriaxone 250 mg IM as a single dose to the mother;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ciprofloxacin 500 mg by mouth as a single dose to her partner(s).

## Conjunctivitis Due To Chlamydia

### Conjunctivitis Due To Chlamydia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give erythromycin by mouth (Table C-9, page C-35) for 14 days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After cleaning the eyes, apply 1% tetracycline ointment to the affected

eye(s) four times daily until the eyes are no longer red, swollen, sticky, or draining pus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother and baby can stay near the health care facility, the baby

does not have to be admitted to the hospital for this treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat the mother and her partner(s) for chlamydia if not already treated

(modify treatment according to local susceptibility, if necessary). Give:

- Dòng dữ liệu: Eyes red, swollen, or draining pus; F-143

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; erythromycin 500 mg by mouth four times daily for seven days to the

mother;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; tetracycline 500 mg by mouth four times daily for seven days to her

partner(s) OR doxycycline 100 mg by mouth twice daily for seven days to her partner(s).

## Gram Stain Or Culture And Sensitivity Not Possible

### Gram Stain Or Culture And Sensitivity Not Possible — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is less than seven days old and has not been treated with

systemic antibiotics before, treat for conjunctivitis due to gonorrhoea (page F-142).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is seven days or older and has been previously treated

unsuccessfully with systemic antibiotics, or if the baby is less than seven days old and the problem is not resolved after 48 hours of treatment, treat for conjunctivitis due to chlamydia (page F-142).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-144; Eyes red, swollen, or draining pus

## Birth Injury F-145

### Birth Injury F-145 — phần 1

This chapter covers birth injuries without visible bleeding. If the baby has a swelling on the scalp, see page F-121. If the baby is pale or bleeding, see page F-113.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s arm and hand are in an abnormal position, lying limply by the

baby’s side (Fig. F-10).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby is unable to wrinkle her/his forehead, close eye on affected side

(Fig. F-11), or suckle without dribbling milk.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s bone (of the arm, leg, or shoulder) is displaced from its

normal position.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There is a swelling over the bone of the baby’s arm, leg, or shoulder.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s arms or legs are not moving symmetrically.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby cries when an arm, leg, or shoulder is touched or moved.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-10; Abnormal position of arm and hand

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-11; Baby unable to wrinkle forehead or close eye on affected

side

### Birth Injury F-145 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-146; Birth injury

Note that babies who were born in a breech position may have fully flexed hips and knees, with the feet near the mouth, or the legs and feet may be to the side of the baby (Fig. F-12). This is the normal resting posture of the baby after birth and is not considered a birth injury.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-12; Normal resting posture of a breech baby

(Table F-2, page F-11), looking especially for a history of a difficult birth, and use the information to determine the probable diagnosis (Table F-28, page F-147).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Birth injury; F-147

## Differential Diagnosis

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE F-28; Differential diagnosis of birth injury

- Dòng dữ liệu: Findingsa; Probable Diagnosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: History; Examination

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Difficult birth; • No spontaneous arm movement; Arm palsy (Erb or

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Breech delivery; on one side; Klumpke), below

• Arm and hand lying limply by baby’s side (Fig. F-10, page F-145) • Large baby (more than 4 kg at birth)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Difficult birth; • Unable to wrinkle forehead,; Facial palsy, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Trauma to face; close eye on affected side, or; F-148

- Dòng dữ liệu: (e.g. use of forceps); suckle without dribbling milk

- Dòng dữ liệu: during birth; (Fig. F-11, page F-145)

• Angle of mouth pulled to one side

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Difficult birth; • Displacement of bone from its; Fracture, page F-148

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 2

normal position • Pain (crying) when a limb or shoulder is moved • Lack of movement or asymmetrical movement of a limb • Swelling over bone a The diagnosis cannot be made if a finding listed in bold is absent. The presence of a finding listed in bold, however, does not guarantee the diagnosis. The diagnosis is definitively confirmed if a finding listed in italics is present. Findings in plain text are supportive findings; their presence helps to confirm the diagnosis, but their absence cannot

be used to rule out the diagnosis.

## Arm Palsy

### Arm Palsy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Handle the baby’s shoulder (e.g. during dressing or when mother is

breastfeeding) gently to prevent further injury, and teach the mother how to do so.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For the first week, reduce pain by strapping the arm in place as described

for a fractured humerus (page F-149).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-148; Birth injury

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is able to care for the baby adequately and there are no other

problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother return with the baby at one week of age:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check for improvement in the baby’s condition;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Advise passive exercises if arm movement is not yet normal.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in two weeks. Explain to the mother that the majority of cases

### Arm Palsy — phần 2

of arm palsy recover by six to nine months of age. If movement of the arm is still limited at one year of age, permanent paralysis is likely.

## Facial Palsy

### Facial Palsy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby cannot close the eye on the affected side, apply ointment to

that eye at least four times daily for as long as the eye does not close. Teach the mother how to do this.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is having difficulty feeding:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Help the mother find a way to help the baby to attach;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is not able to breastfeed, give expressed breast milk

using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in two weeks to verify that the palsy is resolved. Explain to the

mother that most cases of facial palsy resolve spontaneously within two weeks. If movement of the face is still limited at one year of age, permanent paralysis is likely.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm the diagnosis with X-ray, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Handle the baby gently when moving or turning, and teach the mother

how to do so. Avoid movement of the affected limb as much as possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Immobilize the limb to reduce pain when the baby is handled (see below

for how to immobilize specific fractures).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is able to care for the baby and there are no other problems

requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Birth injury; F-149

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the mother that fractures will heal spontaneously, usually

without residual deformity, and that a hard swelling (callus) may be felt over the fracture site at two to three weeks of age. This is part of the normal healing process.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in one month to verify that the fracture has healed. Refer

### General Management — phần 2

babies with unhealed fractures or severe deformities to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre for orthopaedic care, if possible.

## Fractured Humerus

### Fractured Humerus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place a layer of cotton batting or gauze padding between the affected arm

and the chest, from the axilla to the elbow (Fig. F-13).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-13; Splinting a fractured humerus

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Strap the upper arm to the chest using a gauze bandage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Flex the elbow of the affected arm to 90 degrees, and use a separate

bandage to strap the forearm across the abdomen in this position. Ensure that the umbilicus is not covered by the bandage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the fingers twice daily for three days (the baby does not have to be

admitted to the hospital if the mother is able to bring the baby back to the hospital each time):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fingers become blue or swollen, remove the bandage and

rewrap it more loosely;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the bandage is rewrapped, observe the fingers for blueness or

swelling for an additional three days.

### Fractured Humerus — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother return with the baby in 10 days to remove the gauze

bandages.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-150; Birth injury

## Fractured Clavicle

### Fractured Clavicle — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If moving the arm causes the baby to cry, strap the arm in place as

described for a fractured humerus (page F-149).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother return with the baby in five days to remove the gauze

bandages.

## Fractured Femur

### Fractured Femur — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby on her/his back and place a padded splint under the baby

from the waist to below the knee of the affected leg (Fig. F-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE F-14; Splinting a fractured femur

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Strap the splint to the baby by wrapping an elastic bandage around the

waist and from the thigh to below the knee of the affected leg. Ensure that the umbilicus is not covered by the bandage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the toes twice daily for three days (the baby does not have to be

admitted to the hospital if the mother is able to bring the baby back to the hospital each time):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the toes become blue or swollen, remove the bandage and rewrap

it more loosely;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the bandage is rewrapped, observe the toes for blueness or

swelling for an additional three days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the mother return with the baby in 14 days to remove the splint.

## Birth Defects F-151

### Birth Defects F-151 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has a minor birth defect (birth mark, skin tag, extra finger(s) or

toe(s), cleft lip, cleft palate, or club foot).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has a major birth defect (spina bifida/meningomyelocoele,

gastroschisis/omphalocoele, or imperforate anus).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby has a genetic birth defect (e.g. Down syndrome).

## Birth Mark

### Birth Mark — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assure the mother that most birth marks (e.g. capillary haemangioma or

Mongolian blue spot) require no special care and may disappear as the baby gets older.

## Skin Tag Or Extra Finger(S) Or Toe(S)

### Skin Tag Or Extra Finger(S) Or Toe(S) — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Tie off skin tags and extra digits that do not have a bony attachment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the extra digits have a bony attachment, refer the baby to a tertiary

hospital or specialized centre within a few months, if possible, for surgery to remove the extra digits.

## Cleft Lip Or Palate

### Cleft Lip Or Palate — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and reassurance to the mother (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the mother that the most important thing to do at this time is to

feed the baby to ensure adequate growth until surgery can be performed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has a cleft lip but the palate is intact, allow the baby to

attempt to breastfeed:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby breastfeeds successfully and there are no other problems

requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67). Follow up in one week to check growth and weight gain;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby cannot breastfeed well because of the cleft, give

expressed breast milk using an alternative feeding method (page C-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-152; Birth defects

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has a cleft palate, give expressed breast milk using an

alternative feeding method (page C-14).

### Cleft Lip Or Palate — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby is feeding well and gaining weight, refer the baby to a

tertiary hospital or specialized centre, if possible, for surgery to repair the cleft.

## Club Foot

### Club Foot — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and reassurance to the mother (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre within the first

month of life, if possible, for correction of the club foot.

## Spina Bifida/Meningomyelocoele

### Spina Bifida/Meningomyelocoele — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and reassurance to the mother (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the defect is not covered by skin:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Cover with sterile gauze soaked in sterile, normal saline;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Keep gauze moist at all times, and ensure that the baby is kept warm

(page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Organize transfer (page C-63), and refer the baby to a tertiary hospital or

specialized centre for further evaluation or surgical care, if possible.

## Gastroschisis/Omphalocoele

### Gastroschisis/Omphalocoele — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and reassurance to the mother (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby does not receive anything by mouth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the defect is not covered by skin:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Cover with sterile gauze soaked in sterile, normal saline;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Keep gauze moist at all times, and ensure that the baby is kept warm

(page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Birth defects; F-153

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a gastric tube (page P-33), and ensure free drainage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer the baby to a tertiary

hospital or specialized centre for surgery, if possible.

## Imperforate Anus

### Imperforate Anus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and reassurance to the mother (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Establish an IV line (page P-21), and give only IV fluid at maintenance

volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby does not receive anything by mouth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a gastric tube (page P-33) and ensure free drainage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Organize transfer (page C-63), and urgently refer the baby to a tertiary

hospital or specialized centre for surgery, if possible.

## Genetic Birth Defects

### Genetic Birth Defects — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and reassurance to the mother (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has Down syndrome or unusual facial features, advise the

parents about the long-term prognosis, and refer the family to a specialized centre for developmental evaluation and follow-up, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Organize genetic counseling for the parents, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is not going to breastfeed and requests a contraceptive

method, refer her to a family planning service.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-154; Birth defects

## Or Syphilis

### Or Syphilis — phần 1

If a baby is born to a mother with one or more of the problems mentioned in this chapter, there is a higher probability that the baby will develop a problem at some time after birth, even if the baby appears entirely normal at birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s mother had or has:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hepatitis B;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; tuberculosis;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; diabetes;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; syphilis.

## Hepatitis B

### Hepatitis B — phần 1

Mothers who had acute hepatitis during pregnancy or who are carriers of the hepatitis B virus, as demonstrated by a positive serologic test for the hepatitis B surface antigen (HbsAg), may transmit the hepatitis B virus to their babies.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the first dose of hepatitis B vaccine (HBV) (page C-51) 0.5 ml IM

in the upper thigh (page P-15) as soon after birth as possible (preferably within 12 hours of birth).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If available, give hepatitis immune globulin 200 units IM in the other

thigh within 24 hours of birth, or within 48 hours of birth at the latest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassure the mother that it is safe for her to breastfeed her baby.

## Tuberculosis

### Tuberculosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother has active lung tuberculosis and was treated for less

than two months before birth or was diagnosed with tuberculosis after birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not give the tuberculosis vaccine (BCG) at birth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-156; Asymptomatic newborn baby of mother with hepatitis B, tuberculosis, diabetes, or syphilis

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give prophylactic isoniazid 5 mg/kg body weight by mouth once

daily;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; At the age of six weeks, re-evaluate the baby, noting weight gain and

taking an X-ray of the chest, if possible:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are any findings suggestive of active disease, start full

anti-tuberculosis treatment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is doing well and tests are negative, continue

prophylactic isoniazid to complete six months of treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Delay BCG vaccine (page C-51) until two weeks after treatment is

### Tuberculosis — phần 2

completed. If BCG was already given, repeat BCG two weeks after the end of the isoniazid treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassure the mother that it is safe for her to breastfeed her baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in two weeks to assess weight gain.

## Diabetes

### Diabetes — phần 1

Babies of diabetic mothers are at high risk for developing low blood glucose during the first three days of life, even if they are feeding well.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage and support early and frequent breastfeeding, at least eight

times daily, day and night.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is less than three days old, observe the baby until the third

day of age:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure blood glucose (page P-13) at the following times:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; at three hours of age or on admission;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; three hours after the first measurement and then every six hours

for 24 hours or until the blood glucose has been normal for two consecutive days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood glucose is less than 45 mg/dl (2.6 mmol/l), treat for

low blood glucose (page F-91);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood glucose has been normal for three days, the baby is

### Diabetes — phần 2

feeding well, and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is three days old or older and does not show signs of low

blood glucose (e.g. lethargy, jitteriness), observation is not necessary. If

- Dòng dữ liệu: Asymptomatic newborn baby of mother with hepatitis B, tuberculosis, diabetes, or syphilis; F-157

the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the baby (page C-67).

## Syphilis

### Syphilis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother tested positive for syphilis and was treated adequately

(2.4 million units of penicillin) and the treatment started at least 30 days before birth, no treatment is necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother was not treated for syphilis, she was treated

inadequately, or her treatment status is unknown or uncertain and the baby has no signs of syphilis (Table F-10, page F-37):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give the baby procaine benzylpenicillin (or benzathine

benzylpenicillin) IM (Table C-9, pages C-34 to C-35);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give the mother and her partner(s) benzathine benzylpenicillin

1.8 g IM as two injections at separate sites;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Refer the mother and her partner(s) for follow-up to a clinic that

offers services for sexually transmitted infections.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in four weeks to examine the baby for growth and signs of

congenital syphilis.

### Syphilis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Report the case to authorities, if required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-158; Asymptomatic newborn baby of mother with hepatitis B, tuberculosis, diabetes, or syphilis

## Mother With Hiv F-159

### Mother With Hiv F-159 — phần 1

There are no specific signs or features diagnostic of HIV at birth; clinical signs of HIV may begin appearing around six weeks of life, but the baby’s HIV status cannot be verified by antibody testing until 15 to 18 months of age.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The baby’s mother is HIV positive.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When caring for a baby of an HIV-positive mother, always:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Respect the confidentiality of the mother and family;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Care for the baby as for any other baby, paying particular attention

to infection prevention procedures (page C-37);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give the baby all routine immunizations (page C-51).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Emphasize to the mother the importance of condom use to prevent

infection of her partner(s) and transmission of other sexually transmitted infections.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support (page C-57).

## Antiretroviral Therapy

### Antiretroviral Therapy — phần 1

Without antiretroviral therapy, 15% to 30% of babies born to known HIV- positive mothers will be infected during pregnancy and birth, and 5% to 20% may be infected by breastfeeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine if the mother is receiving or has received antiretroviral

treatment for HIV to prevent mother-to-child transmission.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat the baby according to the protocol used for the mother as per

national policy. For example:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If zidovudine (AZT) was given to the mother for four weeks

before birth, continue to give AZT to the baby for six weeks after birth (2 mg/kg body weight by mouth every six hours);

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-160; Mother with HIV

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother received a single dose of nevirapine during labour

and the baby is less than three days old, immediately give the baby nevirapine in suspension 2 mg/kg body weight by mouth;

### Antiretroviral Therapy — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Schedule a follow-up examination in 10 days to assess feeding and

growth.

## Feeding

### Feeding — phần 1

Advise the mother regarding her feeding options, and respect and support the mother’s choice. Allow the mother to make an informed choice about the best feeding option for her baby. Explain to the mother that breastfeeding carries an increased risk of transmitting HIV to the baby after birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inform the mother about her options for feeding, the advantages, and the

risks. The mother can choose to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give replacement feeding if this is acceptable, affordable, feasible,

sustainable, and safe. Explain to the mother that replacement feeding often carries a higher risk of infant mortality than breastfeeding, especially if it cannot be prepared safely, is not continuously available and affordable to the family, and there are limited facilities and water for preparation;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Exclusively breastfeed until replacement feeding is feasible. It is

### Feeding — phần 2

important that the mother stops breastfeeding once replacement feeding is introduced;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Exclusively breastfeed for six months, then continue breastfeeding

while starting complementary feeding (e.g. mashed solid foods) after six months of age.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Help the mother to assess her situation. Help her decide whether to

breastfeed (below) or give replacement feeding (page F-161).

## Mother Chooses To Breastfeed

### Mother Chooses To Breastfeed — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Support the mother’s choice.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the mother against mixed feeding (i.e. feeding anything other than

breast milk, such as commercial breast-milk substitute, animal milks, local porridges, tea, water, etc.). Mixed feeding may increase the risk of both HIV transmission and illness or death from diarrhoea or other illnesses.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Mother with HIV; F-161

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure correct positioning and attachment (page C-12) to prevent

mastitis and damage to the mother’s nipples:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Advise the mother to return immediately if she has any problems

with her breasts or nipples, or if the baby has any difficulty feeding;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge

the baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure a follow-up visit during the first week after discharge to

### Mother Chooses To Breastfeed — phần 2

assess attachment and positioning and the condition of the mother’s breasts;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the baby receives regular follow-up visits with an

appropriate child care provider.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Arrange for further counselling to prepare the mother for the possibility

of stopping breastfeeding early.

## Mother Chooses Replacement Feeding

### Mother Chooses Replacement Feeding — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Support the mother’s choice.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the mother understands that if she chooses replacement

feeding, she should begin complementary feeding at six months of age while continuing to provide milk.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review the guidelines for preparing and feeding breast-milk substitutes

(page C-19).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the mother to begin preparing the replacement feed as soon as she

is able to and teach her how to feed the baby using a cup, cup and spoon, or other device (e.g. paladai; page C-16).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother to feed the baby at least eight times daily. Teach

her to be flexible and respond to the baby’s demands.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the mother written instructions on safe preparation of the

replacement feed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain the risks of replacement feeding and how to avoid them:

### Mother Chooses Replacement Feeding — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The baby may get diarrhoea if the mother’s hands, water, or utensils

are not clean, or if the milk stands too long before being used;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The baby may not grow well if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; too little substitute is given at each feed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; too few feeds are given;

- Dòng dữ liệu: F-162; Mother with HIV

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the substitute contains too much water;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the baby has diarrhoea.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the mother to seek care if the baby has any problems, such as:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; feeding less than six times daily or taking smaller quantities;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; diarrhoea;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; poor weight gain.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no other problems requiring hospitalization, discharge the

baby (page C-67);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure a follow-up visit during the first week after discharge to assess

### Mother Chooses Replacement Feeding — phần 3

how the mother is coping with replacement feeding and ensure that she receives support to provide safe replacement feeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby receives regular follow-up visits with an appropriate

child care provider.

## Maintaining Normal Body Temperature C-1

### Maintaining Normal Body Temperature C-1 — phần 1

A baby who is sick or small (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation) needs additional thermal protection and warmth to maintain normal body temperature. These babies can become hypothermic very quickly, and rewarming the baby can take a long time. The risk of complications and mortality significantly increases if the thermal environment is not optimal.

## General Principles

### General Principles — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby clothed or covered as much as possible at all times,

including during procedures (e.g. when establishing an IV line, during resuscitation):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Clothe the baby and cover the head with a cap or hat;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wrap the baby in a soft dry cloth and cover with a blanket;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Uncover only parts of the body that need observation or treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Care for a sick or small baby in a warm room (not less than 25 °C) that is

free of draughts.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not place the baby near cold objects, such as a wall or window, even

if the baby is in an incubator or under a radiant warmer.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not place the baby directly on a cold surface (e.g. place a cloth or

blanket under the baby before placing on a cold bed or examination table), and ensure that hands are warm before handling the baby.

### General Principles — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby warm during transfer for diagnostic or treatment

procedures. Use warming devices or transfer in skin-to-skin contact (page C-5) with the mother or another person, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure warmth during procedures (e.g. use a radiant warmer).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Change napkins whenever they are wet.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If anything wet is applied to the skin (e.g. moistened gauze), ensure

that the baby is kept warm.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid bathing the baby during the first six hours of life or until the

baby’s temperature is stable; delay bathing a small baby until at least the second day of life.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-2; Maintaining normal body temperature

## Measuring Body Temperature

### Measuring Body Temperature — phần 1

Unless directed otherwise by another chapter, measure the baby’s temperature as often as indicated in Table C-1, below.

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE C-1; Measuring body temperature

- Dòng dữ liệu: Baby with; Small Babya; Very Small; Baby

- Dòng dữ liệu: Serious; Babyb; Progressing

- Dòng dữ liệu: Illness; Well

- Dòng dữ liệu: Frequency of; Every hour; Twice daily; Four times; Once daily

- Dòng dữ liệu: measurements; daily

a Small babies are less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation. b Very small babies are less than 1.5 kg at birth or born before 32 weeks gestation.

### Temperature — phần 1

There are five methods for warming the baby and maintaining the baby’s temperature (Table C-2). See below for specific instructions for using each method.
