the same approach for all families; however, all parents are eager to obtain information about their baby. Keep in mind the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Disbelief, denial, and sadness are normal reactions, especially if the

problem is unexpected. Feelings of unfairness, despair, depression, anxiety, anger, failure, and apprehension are common.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide the parents with all of their options and an honest opinion of the

### Defect — phần 2

likely outcome. Ensure that their decisions are made with informed consent and sufficient understanding of all the possibilities of care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide a bed or cot in the room so that a companion can stay with the

mother if she chooses.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the parents free access to their baby, and keep the baby with the

mother at all times, if possible. The more the parents can do for the baby themselves, the more quickly they will accept the baby as their own.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Help the parents access supportive professional individuals and groups,

when possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Be willing and ready to repeat information and advice on another

occasion, as parents often cannot understand or remember all the messages provided during the first discussion or consultation.

## Baby With Neurodevelopmental Problems

### Baby With Neurodevelopmental Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother to be involved with the care of her baby whenever

possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discuss with the mother the possibility of her caring for her baby at home

once the baby no longer requires treatment or when treatment can be continued at home.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the mother has the support to have the baby receive regular

follow-up visits with an appropriate child care provider.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Communication and emotional support; C-61

## Baby With A Birth Defect

### Baby With A Birth Defect — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother if she would like to see and hold the baby:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Some mothers accept their baby immediately while others may take

longer;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother does not want to see her baby right away, do not

force her to;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Allow the mother to have a companion when she sees her baby for

the first time;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby has major deformities, try to wrap the baby in such a

way that these are covered when the mother first sees the baby;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Point out the normal features of the baby before discussing the

abnormalities;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Allow the mother and family time alone with the baby, if possible

and appropriate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain that nobody is to blame for the abnormality. It may be helpful to

give a simple explanation of why the baby has an abnormality.

### Baby With A Birth Defect — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain the likely prognosis for the baby, but do not dwell on the

negative aspects of the baby’s future.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has a particular birth defect that can be corrected, such

as a cleft palate or club foot, explain this to the mother and reassure her. Do not raise the mother’s hopes, however, if it is not possible to correct the problem.

## Baby Who Is Dying Or Has Died

### Baby Who Is Dying Or Has Died — phần 1

The response of each family to a baby who is dying or has died will be different. Try to apply the following principles:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the mother and family to be with the baby, even during

procedures, if possible and appropriate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain what is being done to the baby and why. Involve the parents in

decision-making when considering whether further treatment is appropriate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an informed decision has been made to stop resuscitation efforts or

if the baby’s death is inevitable, focus on providing emotional support to the family.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-62; Communication and emotional support

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother and family to see and hold the baby after death

and for as long as they desire, if they wish.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Where it is the custom to name babies at birth, encourage the family and

staff to call the baby by the name they have chosen.

### Baby Who Is Dying Or Has Died — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer the mother some mementos of the baby, such as a name tag, a lock

of hair, or a palm print, if culturally appropriate, as this may help with the grieving process.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the family how they will bury the baby. Even if the baby will not be

buried by the family, allow the mother (or a family member) to prepare the baby for the funeral, if she wishes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage locally-accepted burial practices and ensure that medical

procedures (such as autopsies) accommodate them.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Help the family, if necessary, with the paperwork to register the baby’s

death if this is not done by the health care staff.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Arrange to see the family a few weeks after the death:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Answer any questions they have and help them with the grieving

process;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide written information to literate families, if possible,

### Baby Who Is Dying Or Has Died — phần 3

especially if the condition is genetic or steps can be taken to prevent the condition in a future pregnancy.

## Transfer And Referral C-63

### Transfer And Referral C-63 — phần 1

If the baby needs to be transferred to a tertiary hospital or specialized centre, or brought from a more peripheral facility or to a different service within the same facility (e.g. from the delivery room to the newborn special care unit), ensure a safe and timely transfer. It is important to prepare the baby for transfer, communicate with the receiving or sending facility, and provide care during transfer.

## Preparation

### Preparation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the family the reason for transfer of the baby (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain informed consent for any anticipated procedures, if applicable.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfer the mother with the baby, if possible, so that she can continue to

breastfeed or provide expressed breast milk.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the baby for transfer:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the baby’s condition is stable before transfer, if possible;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give necessary treatment before transfer (e.g. treat low blood

glucose), if possible;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is able to feed but the mother is unable to accompany

and breastfeed the baby or the baby must be fed using an alternative feeding method, insert a gastric tube (page P-33);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the IV line, if present, is in place and secured and that

the microdropper is filled with fluid;

### Preparation — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gather essential equipment, supplies, drugs, and fluid (Table C-13,

page C-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have a health care provider (with experience in establishing and

maintaining IV lines, resuscitating a baby, and giving drugs) accompany the baby, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the vehicle has adequate lighting and insulation to keep the

baby warm or to prevent overheating.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask a relative to accompany the baby and mother, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-64; Transfer and referral

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE C-13; Suggested equipment, supplies, drugs, and fluid for transfer

of a sick or small baby

- Dòng dữ liệu: Equipment and Supplies; Drugs and Fluid

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Resuscitation bag and; • IV infusion set; • Any drug (e.g. antibiotics) the

- Dòng dữ liệu: mask; • Butterfly set or; baby is receiving if a dose is

### Preparation — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Suction apparatus; cannula; anticipated during the trip

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Oxygen cylinder with; • Antiseptic solution; • IV fluid

- Dòng dữ liệu: flow metre and a; and cotton-wool balls

- Dòng dữ liệu: headbox, nasal prongs,; • Syringes and needles

- Dòng dữ liệu: nasal catheter, or face; (various sizes and

- Dòng dữ liệu: mask; types)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Stethoscope; • Adhesive tape

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Thermometer; • Gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Blanket; • Napkins (diapers)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Source of warmth; • Food for the babya

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Gastric tubes (5-F and; • Torch and extra

- Dòng dữ liệu: 8-F); batteries and bulb

a If the baby is able to feed and the mother is not accompanying the baby, take expressed breast milk.

## Communication

### Communication — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If receiving the transferred baby, request a referral form (e.g. Fig. A-3,

page A-4) with the baby’s essential information. At discharge or following the death of the baby, send a detailed note or feedback form (e.g. Fig. A-4, page A-5) to the referring facility.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If referring a baby to another facility:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Contact the receiving facility in advance, if possible, so they can be

prepared;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Confirm that the facility is able to admit the baby;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give an estimated time of arrival;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Fill out the referral form and send it with the baby (e.g. Fig. A-3,

page A-4);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the mother is accompanying the baby, inform the facility to

ensure that she will have a place to stay with the baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Transfer and referral; C-65

## Care During Transfer

### Care During Transfer — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby in skin-to-skin contact with the mother or a relative. If

skin-to-skin contact is not possible, keep the baby dressed and covered.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If transferring the baby during cold weather:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use a warming device. Keep the baby dressed and covered, and

check the temperature setting and measure the baby’s temperature every hour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If a warming device is not available, place the baby in a box with

warm water bottles:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Cover the bottles with a cloth and ensure that they are secured

so that they do not directly touch the baby’s skin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When the water becomes cold, refill the bottles with warm water

or remove them from the box.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If transferring the baby during hot weather, ensure that the baby does

not become overheated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby receives feeds or fluid:

### Care During Transfer — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Allow the baby to breastfeed. If the baby cannot be breastfed, give

expressed breast milk by gastric tube (page C-18);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is receiving IV fluid:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Carefully monitor the rate to ensure that the baby receives the

correct volume of fluid (Table C-4, page C-22);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Inspect the infusion site every hour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Look for redness and swelling around the insertion site of

the cannula, which indicate that the cannula is not in the vein and fluid is leaking into the subcutaneous tissue. If redness or swelling is seen at any time, stop the infusion, remove the needle, and establish a new IV line in a different vein;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the volume of fluid infused and compare to the

prescribed volume;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Record all findings.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is receiving oxygen, check the oxygen flow and tubing every

15 minutes.

### Care During Transfer — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-66; Transfer and referral

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby’s respiratory rate every 15 minutes. If the baby is not

breathing at all, is gasping, or has a respiratory rate less than 20 breaths per minute, resuscitate the baby using a bag and mask (page P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Stop the vehicle, if necessary, to manage problems.

## Discharge

### Discharge — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have a written policy on discharging babies. Explain the policy to the

mother and answer any questions she may have.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the baby and confirm that the baby meets the requirements for

discharge. Follow the specific instructions for discharge in each chapter, if applicable.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In general, discharge the baby when the:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; baby is breathing without difficulty and has no other ongoing

problems that cannot be managed on an outpatient basis;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; baby’s body temperature is being maintained in the range of 36.5 °C

to 37.5 °C (using a method that can be continued at home if the baby is small [less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation]);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; mother is confident about her ability to care for the baby;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; baby is breastfeeding well or the mother is confident using an

alternative feeding method;

### Discharge — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; baby is gaining weight.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the mother to return with the baby immediately if the baby has

any problems (e.g. feeding or breathing difficulty, convulsions, abnormal body temperature).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby has received the necessary immunizations (page

C-51).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the mother a sufficient supply of drugs to complete any treatment at

home, or give a prescription for the drugs (e.g. give a three-month supply of iron/folate supplements or a prescription for them).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the mother on home care (normal newborn care and

breastfeeding, proper position for sleeping, danger signs, when and where to go if danger signs occur, etc.), and give her an appointment for a follow-up visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discuss with the mother support systems at home or in the community,

### Discharge — phần 3

especially if mother is adolescent, single, a first-time mother, or HIV positive.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-68; Discharge and follow-up

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Complete the baby’s clinical record with discharge information,

including weight, discharge diagnosis, and the plan for follow-up.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Complete a discharge form (e.g. Fig. A-2, page A-3) and send a

feedback form (e.g. Fig. A-4, page A-5), if necessary, to the referring institution or department.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Write a discharge letter with medical information, instructions for

treatment to be continued at home, and follow-up, if applicable.

## Follow-Up

### Follow-Up — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure at least one follow-up visit after discharge of babies who were

seriously ill, very small (less than 1.5 kg at birth or born before 32 weeks gestation), or fed using an alternative feeding method at the time of discharge. Advise the parents to ensure that the baby has regular paediatric care after the initial follow-up visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At each visit:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess the baby for the specific problem that required follow-up and

ensure that the problem has been resolved;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess the baby’s general condition;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Weigh the baby and assess growth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Advise on and/or manage any problems or concerns identified by the

mother;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess breastfeeding or alternative feeding, and advise the mother

on exclusive breastfeeding;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Reinforce parental education in newborn care and danger signs;

### Follow-Up — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Promote the family’s continued use of the primary health care

facility;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give immunizations (page C-51) if they are due, or refer the baby

and mother to the relevant service.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is HIV positive or the baby is likely to have long-term

problems (e.g. due to brain injury), ensure that the baby receives regular follow-up visits with a child care provider.

## Resuscitating A Baby Who Was Breathing P-1

### Resuscitating A Baby Who Was Breathing P-1 — phần 1

This chapter describes resuscitation of babies with breathing difficulty, and is not intended for use to resuscitate babies immediately after birth.

## Equipment And Supplies

### Equipment And Supplies — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Newborn-size, self-inflating resuscitation bag (an adult-size bag may be

used if a bag specifically for newborn babies is not available)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Newborn-size mask (size 0 for a small baby [less than 2.5 kg at birth or

born before 37 weeks gestation] and size 1 for a normal size baby)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Suction apparatus (e.g. DeLee mucus trap)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Oxygen source (if available)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gather necessary equipment and supplies.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the resuscitation equipment is functioning properly. Block

the mask by making a tight seal with the palm of your hand, and squeeze the bag:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If you feel pressure against your hand, the bag is generating

adequate pressure;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the bag reinflates when you release your grip, the bag is

functioning properly.

### Equipment And Supplies — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby wrapped or covered, except for the face and upper chest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Move the baby to a firm, warm surface under a radiant warmer.

## Opening The Airway

### Opening The Airway — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Position the baby (Fig. P-1, page P-2):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the baby on her/his back;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Position the baby’s head in a slightly extended position to open the

airway (the neck should not be as extended as for adults). A rolled-

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-2; Resuscitating a baby who was breathing

up piece of cloth under the baby’s shoulders may be used to extend the head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-1; Correct position of the head for ventilation

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If mucus or vomitus is present, clear the airway by suctioning first the

mouth and then the nostrils.

Do not suction deep in the throat as this may decrease the baby’s heart rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is still not breathing, ventilate with a bag and mask.

## Ventilating With A Bag And Mask

### Ventilating With A Bag And Mask — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Recheck the baby’s position and ensure that the neck is slightly extended

(Fig. P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Position the mask and check the seal (Fig. P-2, page P-3):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the mask on the baby’s face so that it covers the baby’s chin,

mouth, and nose;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Form a seal between the mask and the baby’s face;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Squeeze the bag with two fingers only (adult-size bag) or with the

whole hand (newborn-size bag);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the seal between the mask and the baby’s face by ventilating

two times and observing the rise of the chest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Resuscitating a baby who was breathing; P-3

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-2; Positioning the mask and checking the seal

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once a seal is ensured and chest movement is present, ventilate the baby

using oxygen. If oxygen is not immediately available, begin ventilating using room air.

### Ventilating With A Bag And Mask — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Maintain the correct rate (approximately 40 breaths per minute) and

pressure during ventilation:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s chest is rising, ventilation pressure is probably

adequate;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s chest is not rising:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Recheck and correct, if necessary, the position of the baby

(Fig. P-1, page P-2);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Reposition the mask on the baby’s face to improve the seal

between the mask and the baby’s face;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Squeeze the bag harder to increase ventilation pressure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ventilate for one minute and then stop and quickly determine if the baby

is breathing spontaneously:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the respiratory rate is normal (30 to 60 breaths per minute), stop

ventilating;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is central cyanosis (blue tongue and lips), chest indrawing,

### Ventilating With A Bag And Mask — phần 3

or grunting on expiration, or the respiratory rate is 20 to 30 or more than 60 breaths per minute, treat for breathing difficulty (page F-47);

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-4; Resuscitating a baby who was breathing

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is gasping or not breathing, or the respiratory rate is

less than 20 breaths per minute, continue ventilating.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby starts crying, stop ventilating and observe the baby’s

respiratory rate for five minutes after crying stops:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the respiratory rate is normal (30 to 60 breaths per minute), stop

ventilating;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is central cyanosis (blue tongue and lips), chest indrawing,

or grunting on expiration, or the respiratory rate is 20 to 30 or more than 60 breaths per minute, treat for breathing difficulty (page F-47);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is gasping or not breathing, or the respiratory rate is

### Ventilating With A Bag And Mask — phần 4

less than 20 breaths per minute, continue ventilating.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is not breathing regularly after 20 minutes of ventilation:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Continue ventilation with oxygen;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Establish an IV line (page P-21), if one is not already in place, and

give a bolus of IV fluid 10 ml/kg body weight over 10 minutes, and then continue IV fluid at maintenance volume according to the baby’s age (Table C-4, page C-22);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Insert a gastric tube (page P-33) to empty the stomach of air and

secretions;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Organize transfer (page C-63), and refer the baby to a tertiary

hospital or specialized centre for further evaluation, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is no gasping or breathing at all after 20 minutes of

ventilation, or gasping but no breathing after 30 minutes of ventilation, stop ventilating. Provide emotional support to the family (page C-57).

## Care After Successful Resuscitation

### Care After Successful Resuscitation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby under a radiant warmer until the baby’s condition is stable.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s respiratory rate and look for other signs of illness

every hour for four hours and then every two hours for the next 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If signs of breathing difficulty recur (respiratory rate consistently more

than 60 or less than 30 breaths per minute, central cyanosis, chest indrawing, or grunting on expiration), treat for breathing difficulty (page F-47).

## Measuring Body Temperature P-5

### Measuring Body Temperature P-5 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; thermometer that measures temperatures as low as 35 °C (axillary

temperature)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; thermometer that measures temperatures as low as 25 °C (rectal

temperature)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; disinfectant solution (Table C-10, page C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; water-based lubricant

## Measuring Axillary Temperature

### Measuring Axillary Temperature — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a regular thermometer that measures temperatures as low as 35 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the thermometer is clean.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby as warm as possible during the procedure (e.g. warmly

wrapped or on a warm surface).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby on her/his back or side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Shake the thermometer until it is below 35 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the tip of the thermometer high in the apex of the baby’s axilla and

hold the arm continuously against the body for at least three minutes (Fig. P-3, page P-6).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the thermometer and read the temperature. If the temperature is

too low to be recorded by this thermometer (less than 35 °C), measure rectal temperature (page P-6).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wipe the thermometer with a disinfectant solution after use.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-6; Measuring body temperature

### Measuring Axillary Temperature — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-3; Measuring axillary temperature

## Measuring Rectal Temperature

### Measuring Rectal Temperature — phần 1

Only measure rectal temperature if the temperature is too low to be recorded with a regular thermometer in the axilla.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a thermometer that measures temperatures as low as 25 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the thermometer is clean.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby as warm as possible during the procedure (e.g. warmly

wrapped or on a warm surface).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby on her/his back or side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Shake the thermometer until it is below 25 °C.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lubricate the thermometer using a water-based lubricant.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently grasp the baby’s ankles and hold the legs in knee-chest position

(Fig. P-4).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-4; Measuring rectal temperature

- Dòng dữ liệu: Measuring body temperature; P-7

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the thermometer in the rectum (Fig. P-4, page P-6) to a maximum

### Measuring Rectal Temperature — phần 2

depth of 2 cm and hold it in place for at least three minutes.

Do not leave the baby alone with the thermometer in the rectum; any movement of the baby may result in the thermometer perforating the rectum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the thermometer and read the temperature.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wipe the thermometer with a disinfectant solution after use.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-8; Measuring body temperature

## Taking Blood Samples P-9

### Taking Blood Samples P-9 — phần 1

Determine how much blood will be needed to perform all necessary laboratory investigations (e.g. haemoglobin, blood glucose, serum bilirubin, and blood type and cross-match) and take enough blood at one time for all the tests, if possible. If only a small volume of blood is needed (e.g. for measurement of blood glucose, serum bilirubin, or haemoglobin), use a capillary blood sample (heel prick) if possible (page P-11). If a larger

volume of blood is needed than can be obtained from a heel prick (e.g. when more than 1 ml of blood is needed for several laboratory investigations or for blood culture and sensitivity), use venepuncture (below).

## Venepuncture

### Venepuncture — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use veins in the hands and feet first. Do not use jugular or femoral

veins for routine sampling.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A closed system using a butterfly set and syringe ensures a sterile blood

sample to use for bacterial culture and sensitivity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic solution (Table C-10,

page C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; dry cotton-wool ball

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile needle (21- to 23-gauge) or butterfly set (23- to 25-gauge)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile syringe (of appropriate size for the amount of blood needed; a

syringe is not required if only a needle is used)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; appropriate blood collection tubes

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow principles of infection prevention (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the vein to be used.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

### Venepuncture — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the skin over the vein using a swab or cotton-wool ball soaked

in antiseptic solution, and allow to dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-10; Taking blood samples

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the blood collection tubes are within easy reach.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have an assistant use her/his forefinger and thumb to gently encircle the

limb above the site selected for puncture.

## Needle With A Syringe Or Butterfly Set

### Needle With A Syringe Or Butterfly Set — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Attach the syringe to the needle or butterfly set tubing.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert the needle through the skin at an angle of about 15 degrees, with

the bevel of the needle facing upward.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pull gently on the syringe plunger as the needle is advanced. Once

blood flows easily into the syringe or the tubing of the butterfly set, do not advance the needle any further.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take enough blood to perform all necessary laboratory investigations.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After blood is collected:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have the assistant remove her/his finger and thumb from around

the baby’s limb;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Withdraw the needle from the vein, and have the assistant apply

gentle pressure to the puncture site with a dry cotton-wool ball for several minutes to prevent bruising.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an open collection tube is used, carefully recap the needle (page

### Needle With A Syringe Or Butterfly Set — phần 2

C-42) and remove it from the syringe before transferring the blood into the tube.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dispose of the syringe (or clean it) and the butterfly set or needle

according to recommended infection prevention procedures (page C-42).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Record the volume of blood taken.

## Needle Without Syringe

### Needle Without Syringe — phần 1

A needle can be used without a syringe; however, this can be messy and is unsterile, making this method unsuitable for culture and sensitivity. Ensure that the needle is disposed of properly (page C-42).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert the needle through the skin at an angle of about 15 degrees, with

the bevel of the needle facing upward, until blood flows out quickly:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the blood comes out very slowly, gently adjust the needle slightly

by pulling it back or pushing it in;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Taking blood samples; P-11

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Hold the collection tubes under the needle to collect the blood, being

careful not to touch the tubes or the end of the needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take enough blood to perform all necessary laboratory investigations.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After blood is collected, withdraw the needle from the vein, and have the

### Needle Without Syringe — phần 2

assistant apply gentle pressure to the puncture site with a dry cotton-wool ball for several minutes to prevent bruising.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dispose of the needle according to recommended infection prevention

procedures (page C-42).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Record the volume of blood taken.

## Capillary Blood Sample (Heel Prick)

### Capillary Blood Sample (Heel Prick) — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic solution (Table C-10, page

C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; dry cotton-wool ball

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile lancet (if a lancet is not available, use a 24-gauge needle)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; capillary tubes or other appropriate glass collection tubes

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow principles of infection prevention (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the skin of the heel using a swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in

antiseptic solution, and allow to dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Flex the foot up towards the leg and hold it in this position with one

hand.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Squeeze the heel firmly enough to make it flush red (but not so much

that it turns white).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Puncture the skin (about 1 to 2 mm deep) firmly with a lancet:

### Capillary Blood Sample (Heel Prick) — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-12; Taking blood samples

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Aim towards the lateral or medial side of the heel (Fig. P-5);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Avoid the heel pad because of the risk of infection;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Avoid using previously used sites, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-5; Site for heel prick

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Squeeze the heel gently and intermittently to enhance blood flow. Avoid

excessive squeezing and rubbing of the heel, as this will cause bruising and dilution of blood with tissue fluid, giving an inaccurate result.

A tiny jab is more unpleasant for the baby because it will take longer to collect the blood and requires prolonged squeezing of the heel; in some cases, a second heel stick may be required. Excessively deep heel sticks, however, can cause cuts, infection, and scarring.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Collect blood into the tube, taking enough blood to perform all necessary

laboratory investigations.

### Capillary Blood Sample (Heel Prick) — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After blood is collected, have an assistant apply gentle pressure to the

puncture site with a dry cotton-wool ball for several minutes to prevent bruising.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Record the volume of blood taken.

## Measuring Blood Glucose P-13

### Measuring Blood Glucose P-13 — phần 1

Measure blood glucose using standard laboratory methods. If laboratory methods are not available, use paper reagent strips that are made for newborn babies. Note that paper reagent strips may underestimate blood glucose by as much as 0.5 to 1.0 mmol/l.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gather necessary equipment and supplies.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Read the instructions for the reagent strips.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take a blood sample (page P-9).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfer the required volume of blood onto the reagent strip, usually in

an area at the tip of the strip, taking care to cover all the required area with one or two drops of blood at the same time.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Leave the blood on the strip for the required length of time—usually 60

seconds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wipe or wash off the blood, using running water, according to the

manufacturer’s instructions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Estimate the blood glucose:

### Measuring Blood Glucose P-13 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Immediately compare the colour on the strip to a colour chart

(usually on the container of the reagent strips) to estimate blood glucose;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Improve the accuracy of this method by using a reflectance metre

(supplied by the manufacturer of the reagent strips), if available, to read the colour change on the reagent strip.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the blood glucose is less than 25 mg/dl (1.1 mmol/l), confirm by

laboratory measurement, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-14; Measuring blood glucose

## General Principles

### General Principles — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The sites for IM injections include the:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Quadriceps muscle group of the upper, outer thigh. This site is

preferred because of the small risk of giving the injection intravenously, hitting the femur with the needle, or injuring the sciatic nerve;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gluteus muscle group in the buttock. This muscle group is difficult

to use for IM injection because of variable amounts of fat and subcutaneous tissue and the danger of injury to the sciatic nerve and major blood vessels in the region. If using this site, use only the upper, outer quadrant of the muscle, and always aspirate before injecting;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Deltoid muscle group. This site can be used for giving

immunizations but should not be used for giving other injections.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Minimize pain with injection by:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Using a sharp needle of the smallest diameter that will allow fluid to

### General Principles — phần 2

flow freely (e.g. 22- to 24-gauge);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensuring that no material for injection is in the needle at the time of

insertion into the skin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Using a minimal volume for injection (e.g. 2 ml or less at any single

injection site);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Avoiding rapid injection of material;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Using alternative injection sites for subsequent injections.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Potential complications of IM injections include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; inadvertent intra-arterial or intravenous injection;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; infection from contaminated injection material;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; neural injury (typically the sciatic nerve after injections in the

buttock);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; local tissue damage due to injection of irritants.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-16; Giving injections

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid these complications by:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; selecting the safest agents for injection;

### General Principles — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; choosing the proper injection site;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; establishing anatomic landmarks;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cleansing the skin thoroughly;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; alternating sites for subsequent injections;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; aspirating before injection;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; avoiding tracking the drug into superficial tissues;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; using a needle of adequate length to reach the intended injection site.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 1-inch needle of the smallest size that will allow fluid to flow

freely (e.g. 22- to 24-gauge)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile syringe of the smallest size available that has adequate markings

for proper dose (e.g. 1- to 3-ml)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; dry cotton-wool ball

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Select the site for injection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Draw the material for injection into the syringe.

### General Principles — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the drug and dose are correct.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the centre of the target muscle between the thumb and forefinger,

if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert the needle at a 90-degree angle through the skin with a single

quick motion (Fig. P-6, page P-17).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Giving injections; P-17

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-6; Intramuscular injection into quadriceps muscle group

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Withdraw the plunger of the syringe slightly to ensure that the tip of the

needle is not in a vein (i.e. no blood should enter the needle):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the needle is in a vein:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Withdraw the needle without injecting the material;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Apply gentle pressure to the site with a dry cotton-wool ball to

prevent bruising;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place a new, sterile needle on the syringe;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Choose a new site for injection;

### General Principles — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Repeat the procedure described above.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the needle is in the muscle, inject the material with steady

pressure for three to five seconds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Upon completion of the injection, withdraw the needle and apply gentle

pressure with a dry cotton-wool ball.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Record the site of the injection, and rotate the site of subsequent injections.

## Intravenous (Iv) Injections

### Intravenous (Iv) Injections — phần 1

The directions in this section are for giving an IV push injection to a baby with an IV line in place; these directions do not apply if the drug is mixed with IV fluid in a bag and then infused.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic solution (Table C-10, page

C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-18; Giving injections

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; two sterile 1-inch needles of the smallest size that will allow fluid to flow

freely (e.g. 22- to 24-gauge)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; two sterile syringes of the smallest size available that have adequate

markings for proper dose (e.g. 1- to 3-ml)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; 2 ml IV fluid

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Choose the place in the IV line where an IV injection can be given

closest to the insertion site of the cannula (e.g. a valve or a soft rubber connector).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clean the port with the swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic

### Intravenous (Iv) Injections — phần 2

solution, and allow to dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Draw the material for injection into the syringe.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the drug and dose are correct.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the IV fluid was infusing without problem:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Stop the IV infusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Insert the needle into the IV line, and inject the material slowly over

two minutes, carefully observing the area around the cannula for swelling.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is any question as to whether the cannula is properly

positioned in the vein:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Stop the IV infusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Flush the IV line first with 2 ml of IV fluid, observing the area

around the cannula carefully for swelling that indicates that the cannula has come out of the vein;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cannula is still in the vein, inject the material slowly over two

minutes, carefully observing the area around the cannula for swelling.

### Intravenous (Iv) Injections — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Upon completion of the injection, withdraw the needle and restart the IV

infusion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Giving injections; P-19

## Intradermal Injections

### Intradermal Injections — phần 1

Only use intradermal injection for the BCG vaccine (page C-51) and when first administering local anaesthetic for draining an abscess (page P-43).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 25- or 27-gauge, 5/8-inch needle

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 21-gauge, 1-inch needle

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile tuberculin syringe (1-ml)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; dry cotton-wool ball

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Select the site for injection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Draw the material for injection into the syringe using the 21-gauge needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the correct drug and dose are given.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Replace the 21-gauge needle with a 25- or 27-gauge needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the syringe and needle almost parallel with the skin, with the bevel

of the needle facing up.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pull the skin taut with one hand, and insert the tip of the needle barely

### Intradermal Injections — phần 2

under the skin. Advance the needle slowly until the bevel of the needle has fully entered the skin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently point the needle upward, without repiercing the skin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject the material with steady pressure for three to five seconds (there

will be significant resistance) and look for a blanching of the skin. The baby will probably cry during the injection; a true intradermal injection often burns slightly and should raise a small “bleb” under the skin that causes the skin to pucker like the skin of an orange (peau d’orange).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Upon completion of the injection, withdraw the needle and apply gentle

pressure with a dry cotton-wool ball.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-20; Giving injections

## Establishing An Intravenous Line P-21

### Establishing An Intravenous Line P-21 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Various sites can be used to establish an intravenous (IV) line. Common

sites used for a baby are:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; peripheral veins on the back of the hand or top of the foot (the most

common and preferred sites);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; veins on the forearm, the front of the elbow, or around the ankle or

knee (minimize use of the veins around the knee because there is a greater risk of the needle coming in contact with the bone);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; scalp veins.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a peripheral IV line cannot be established quickly in an emergency

situation, use an umbilical vein catheter (page P-24) or intraosseous line (page P-27).

## Peripheral Iv Line

### Peripheral Iv Line — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic solution (Table C-10, page

C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile infusion set with IV fluid (use a microdropper if one is available)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile butterfly set or cannula (23- to 25-gauge; if the IV line is

required for a blood transfusion, ensure that the needle is large enough [e.g. 22-gauge] so that the blood does not clot in the needle during the transfusion)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; adhesive strapping or thin paper tape

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; tincture of benzoin (if available)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; rubber band (if using a scalp vein)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; arm board (or splint)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow principles of infection prevention (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-22; Establishing an intravenous line

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the solution to be infused, ensuring that the entire infusion set is

### Peripheral Iv Line — phần 2

filled with fluid and that there is no air in the infusion set. If a butterfly set is used, ensure that the set is filled with IV fluid.

Air embolism can occur easily in babies. It is essential to ensure that all components of the IV infusion set are filled with fluid and that there are no air bubbles in the set before beginning the infusion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the skin over the vein using a swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in

antiseptic solution, and allow to dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have an assistant press on the skin near the vein to act as a tourniquet:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If using a vein on the hand, foot, arm, or leg, have the assistant

use her/his forefinger and thumb to gently encircle the limb above the chosen site of insertion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If using a scalp vein, have an assistant press over the vein below the

### Peripheral Iv Line — phần 3

chosen site of insertion, or place a rubber band (as a tourniquet) around the baby’s head (Fig. P-7).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-7; Using a rubber band as a tourniquet for scalp vein

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert the needle at a 15-degree angle through the skin, with the bevel of

the needle facing upward:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If using a butterfly set, a small amount of blood will flush back into

the tubing when the vein is punctured. Do not push the needle in any further;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If using a cannula:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once blood fills the hub of the cannula, withdraw the needle

partially while continuing to push the cannula in;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Establishing an intravenous line; P-23

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When the hub of the cannula reaches the skin at the puncture

site, withdraw the needle completely;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Dispose of the needle according to recommended infection

prevention procedures (page C-42).

### Peripheral Iv Line — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the assistant remove her/his finger and thumb from around the

baby’s limb (or remove the rubber band if a scalp vein was used).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Connect the infusion set to the cannula or butterfly set:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that there are no air bubbles in the infusion set;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Infuse fluid into the vein for a few seconds to make sure that the vein

has been successfully cannulated. The fluid should run freely, and there should be no swelling around the site of the cannula;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If swelling develops around the site of infusion, withdraw the

needle from the vein and repeat the procedure using a different vein.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If using a vein in the hand, arm, foot, or leg, immobilize the limb (e.g.

using an arm board [or splint] and adhesive strapping or thin paper tape) to minimize movement (e.g. Fig. P-8).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-8; Immobilizing the hand

### Peripheral Iv Line — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Secure the cannula or butterfly set in position using strips of adhesive

strapping or thin paper tape (e.g. Fig. P-9, page P-24). If tincture of benzoin is available, apply this to the skin before applying the adhesive strapping.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-24; Establishing an intravenous line

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-9; Securing butterfly set in place

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inspect the infusion site every hour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Look for redness and swelling around the insertion site of the

cannula, which indicate that the cannula is not in the vein and fluid is leaking into the subcutaneous tissue. If redness or swelling is seen at any time, stop the infusion, remove the needle, and establish a new IV line in a different vein;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the volume of fluid infused and compare to the prescribed

volume;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Record all findings.

### Peripheral Iv Line — phần 6

Solutions containing glucose can cause tissue to die and should not be allowed to leak into subcutaneous tissue.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Change the IV infusion set and fluid bag every 24 hours, even if the bag

still contains IV fluid (they can be a major source of infection).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Establishing an intravenous line; P-25

## Umbilical Vein Catheter

### Umbilical Vein Catheter — phần 1

An umbilical vein catheter is indicated only when the need for IV access is urgent but a peripheral IV line cannot be established quickly.

## Equipment And Supplies

### Equipment And Supplies — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; high-level disinfected or sterile gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; high-level disinfected or sterile umbilical catheter or ordinary gastric tube:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if the baby weighs less than 1.5 kg, use a 3.5-F catheter

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if the baby weighs 1.5 kg or more, use a 5-F catheter

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile infusion set with IV fluid (use a microdropper if one is available)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 5- or 10-ml syringe

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; swabs or cotton-wool balls soaked in antiseptic solution (Table C-10,

page C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile drapes

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile blade

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; cord tie or suture (to control bleeding)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile forceps

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile suture, adhesive strapping, or thin paper tape (to secure catheter)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gather necessary equipment and supplies.

### Equipment And Supplies — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow principles of infection prevention (page C-37) and aseptic

technique (page C-40).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the solution to be infused.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the umbilicus and surrounding skin by washing in an outward

spiral motion with a swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-26; Establishing an intravenous line

solution. Repeat two more times, using a new swab or cotton-wool ball each time, and allow to dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove examination gloves and put on high-level disinfected or sterile

gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Fill the umbilical catheter with IV fluid using a closed syringe (i.e. with

the plunger completely inside the barrel of the syringe) attached to the end of the catheter.

### Equipment And Supplies — phần 3

Ensure that air is not in the catheter and that a closed syringe is attached to the end of the catheter; a sudden deep breath by the baby just after the catheter has been inserted may result in an air embolus if air is inside the catheter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place sterile drapes over the baby’s body so that only the umbilical area

is exposed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place a cord tie or suture around the base of the umbilicus to control

bleeding, and using a sterile blade, cut the cord to a length of 1 to 2 cm (Fig. P-10A).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the two umbilical arteries, which are thicker-walled and usually

contracted, and the single umbilical vein, which usually has a wider opening and is found above the arteries (closer to the baby’s head; Fig. P-10).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-10; Inserting an umbilical vein catheter

- Dòng dữ liệu: Establishing an intravenous line; P-27

### Equipment And Supplies — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the catheter in one hand (applying gentle traction to the cord with

forceps in the other hand, if necessary) and insert the catheter into the umbilical vein, guiding the catheter towards the head of the baby and to the baby’s right side (Fig. P-10B, page P-26).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; As the catheter is advanced, periodically apply gentle suction with the

syringe until blood flows back. Once blood flows back freely through the catheter (usually after the catheter is inserted 5 to 7 cm), do not advance the catheter any further.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If resistance is encountered while advancing the catheter, especially

in the first 2 to 3 cm, do not continue. Remove the catheter and try again.

Never force the umbilical catheter if resistance is encountered.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Tie the cord tie or suture around the stump of the umbilicus to hold the

### Equipment And Supplies — phần 5

catheter in place and prevent bleeding around the catheter or from one of the arteries.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the syringe and connect the infusion set to the catheter, ensuring

that there are no air bubbles in the set.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Secure the catheter with suture material or adhesive tape (Fig. P-10C,

page P-26) to prevent it from being dislodged.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inspect the infusion every hour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Look for redness and swelling around the umbilicus, which may

indicate infection. If redness or swelling is seen at any time, stop the infusion and remove the umbilical vein catheter. Attempt to establish a peripheral IV line again, and treat for infection of the umbilicus (page F-135);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the volume of fluid infused and compare to the prescribed

volume;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Record all findings.

## Intraosseous Infusion

### Intraosseous Infusion — phần 1

Establishing intravenous access in a newborn baby can be difficult. In an emergency, a good temporary alternative is the intraosseous route using the bone marrow cavity. Fluid and drugs can be given by this route.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-28; Establishing an intravenous line

Remove the intraosseous line as soon as other IV access is established (within eight hours, if possible). Do not place an intraosseous line if there is infection at the intended insertion site or if the bone is fractured. Because this procedure is only performed in an emergency, no anaesthetic is required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic solution (Table C-10, page

C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile intraosseous needle, bone marrow needle, or 22-gauge needle

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile infusion set with IV fluid (use a microdropper if one is available)

### Intraosseous Infusion — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; adhesive strapping or thin paper tape

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 5-ml syringe

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; elastic bandage

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; padded splint

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the solution to be infused, ensuring that the entire infusion set is

filled with fluid and that there is no air in the infusion set.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If using a regular hypodermic needle, attach a 5-ml syringe filled with

3 ml of IV fluid, and flush the fluid through the needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the insertion site (proximal end of tibia or distal end of femur):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The site at the proximal end of the tibia is 1 cm below and 1 cm

medial to the tibial tuberosity;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The site at the distal end of the femur is 2 cm above the lateral

condyle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

### Intraosseous Infusion — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the skin over the insertion site using a swab or cotton-wool ball

soaked in antiseptic solution, and allow to dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Establishing an intravenous line; P-29

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Position the baby’s leg with the knee bent about 30 degrees and the heel

resting on the table.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Support the upper tibia with one hand, placed so that the hand is not

directly behind the site of insertion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the needle (with the attached syringe if using a hypodermic needle)

in the other hand at a 90-degree angle to the selected insertion site, angled slightly towards the foot.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advance the needle using a firm, twisting motion and moderate,

controlled force. Stop immediately when there is a sudden decrease in resistance to the needle, which indicates that the needle has entered the marrow cavity.

### Intraosseous Infusion — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the needle is properly positioned, remove the stylet (if a bone

marrow or intraosseous needle was used) and attach the syringe.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Aspirate using the syringe to confirm that the needle is correctly

positioned. The aspirate should look like blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Slowly inject 3 ml of IV fluid to check for proper placement of needle:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Look for swelling (indicating leaking of fluid under the skin) at the

front of the leg or in the calf muscle at the back of the leg. If swelling is seen, remove the needle and try again;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If it is difficult to infuse the fluid but there is no swelling in the

calf muscle, the needle may have entered the posterior bone cortex. Withdraw the needle approximately 0.5 cm and cautiously inject IV fluid again.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If no problems are detected, attach the infusion set to the needle (Fig.

P-11).

### Intraosseous Infusion — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-11; Intraosseous infusion

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-30; Establishing an intravenous line

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Secure the needle in place using tape, and splint the leg as for a fractured

femur (page F-150), ensuring that the elastic bandage does not interfere with the needle or infusion set.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inspect the infusion site every hour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Look for redness and swelling around the insertion site of the

cannula and in the baby’s calf muscle, which indicate that the cannula is not in the vein and fluid is leaking into the subcutaneous tissue. If redness or swelling is seen at any time, stop the infusion, remove the needle, and attempt to establish a peripheral IV line again or establish a new intraosseous line at a different site;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the volume of fluid infused and compare to the prescribed

### Intraosseous Infusion — phần 6

volume; flow rates may alter dramatically with changes in the position of the leg;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Record all findings.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the intraosseous needle as soon as alternative IV access is

available, and within eight hours, if possible.

## Monitoring The Transfused Baby

### Monitoring The Transfused Baby — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For each transfusion, monitor the baby at the following stages:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; before starting the transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; at the onset of the transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; every five minutes for the first 15 minutes after starting the

transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; at least every hour during the transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; every four hours for 24 hours after completing the transfusion.

Closely monitor the baby during the first 15 minutes of the transfusion and regularly thereafter to detect early signs of a transfusion reaction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At each of these stages, record the following information on the baby’s

chart:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; general appearance;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; temperature;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; heart rate;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; respiratory rate;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fluid balance (i.e. oral and IV fluid intake and urine output).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In addition, record:

### Monitoring The Transfused Baby — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the time the transfusion is started and completed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the volume and type of all blood transfused;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the unique donation numbers of all blood transfused;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; any adverse effects.

## Transfusing Blood

### Transfusing Blood — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review the general principles of the clinical use of blood (page C-47).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an IV line is not yet in place, establish an IV line (page P-21).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-32; Transfusing blood

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Before beginning the transfusion, check (with a second staff member, if

possible) to ensure that the:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; blood is the correct type for the baby, the baby’s information is

clearly marked, and the blood has been matched against the blood of the mother and the baby. In emergency situations, use type O, Rh- negative blood;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; blood transfusion bag has not been opened and does not leak;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; blood pack has not been out of the refrigerator for more than two

hours, the plasma is not pink, the red cells do not look purple or black, and the blood is not clotted;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; IV line is patent and the needle used is large enough (e.g. 22-gauge)

### Transfusing Blood — phần 2

so that the blood does not clot in the needle during the transfusion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Record the baby’s temperature and heart and respiratory rates.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the protective cover from the blood bag or bottle without

touching the opening, and attach a blood infusion set.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the clamp on the tubing of the blood infusion set, allow blood to

run through to the end of tubing, and then close the clamp.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Detach the tubing at the infusion site and immediately attach the tubing

from the blood transfusion set.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfuse whole blood at the rate of 20 ml/kg body weight over four

hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s temperature and heart and respiratory rates, and slow

the infusion to half the rate when the baby’s vital signs begin to improve.

Do not leave a unit of blood hanging for more than four hours.

### Transfusing Blood — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use an infusion device to control the rate of transfusion, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the blood is flowing at the correct rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Upon completion of the transfusion, reassess the baby. If another

transfusion is required, transfuse blood at the same rate and volume.

## Inserting A Gastric Tube P-33

### Inserting A Gastric Tube P-33 — phần 1

A gastric tube may be inserted via one nostril or the mouth. Insert the tube via the nostril if the baby is breathing regularly, using the smallest (narrowest) tube available. Insert the tube via the mouth if the tube is needed for drainage of the stomach, for feeding a baby with breathing difficulty, or if only a relatively large tube is available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean plastic tube or catheter appropriate for baby’s weight:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if the baby weighs less than 2 kg, use a 5-F tube

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if the baby weighs 2 kg or more, use an 8-F tube

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; writing pen or flexible tape measure

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; 3- to 5-ml syringe (for aspiration)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; blue litmus paper or stethoscope

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile or high-level disinfected syringe or funnel suitable for holding

breast milk (if the tube will be used for feeding)

### Inserting A Gastric Tube P-33 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; cap for gastric tube (if the tube will be used for feeding)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; adhesive strapping

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; tincture of benzoin (if available)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Estimate the required length of tube:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Hold the tube so that it mimics the route that it will follow once

inserted (i.e. from the mouth or the tip of the nostril to the lower tip of the ear lobe and then to the stomach, just below the rib margin; Fig. P-12, page P-34), and place a mark on the tube with a pen or a piece of strapping;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Alternatively, estimate the distance using a flexible tape measure,

and mark the distance on the tube with a pen or a piece of strapping.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-34; Inserting a gastric tube

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-12; Measuring gastric tube for oral (A) and nasal (B) routes

### Inserting A Gastric Tube P-33 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Flex the baby’s neck slightly and gently pass the tube through the mouth

(Fig. P-13) or through one nostril to the required distance. If using the nasal route:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If a nasal catheter is in place for administration of oxygen, insert

the gastric tube through the same nostril, if possible;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the tube does not slide easily into the nostril, try the other

nostril;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the tube still does not slide easily into the nostril, use the oral

route.

Never force the gastric tube into the nostril if resistance is encountered.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-13; Inserting oral gastric tube

- Dòng dữ liệu: Inserting a gastric tube; P-35

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Secure the tube in position with adhesive strapping (Fig. P-14):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If tincture of benzoin is available, apply this to the skin first before

applying the adhesive strapping;

### Inserting A Gastric Tube P-33 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If a nasogastric tube is used, avoid pulling the tube taut against the

nostril, as this may injure the skin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-14; Securing oral (A) and nasal (B) gastric tube in place

## Confirming Proper Placement Of Gastric Tube

### Confirming Proper Placement Of Gastric Tube — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm proper placement of the tube:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Fill a syringe with 1 to 2 ml of air and connect it to the end of the

tube. Use a stethoscope to listen over the stomach as air is quickly injected into the tube:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If a whistling sound is heard through the stethoscope as the

air is injected, the end of the tube is correctly positioned in the stomach;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If a whistling sound is not heard, the tube is not properly

positioned. Remove the tube and repeat the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Alternatively, test the acidity of the aspirate:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Note that this method is only suitable for babies more than 24

hours old or small babies (less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation) who are more than 48 hours old;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use a syringe to aspirate some fluid, and place a drop of fluid

onto a strip of blue litmus paper:

### Confirming Proper Placement Of Gastric Tube — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-36; Inserting a gastric tube

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the litmus paper turns pink, the fluid is acidic and the

tip of the tube is correctly positioned in the stomach;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the litmus paper remains blue, the tip of the tube is not

in the correct position. Remove the tube and repeat the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Replace the tube with another clean gastric tube after three days, or

earlier if it is pulled out or becomes blocked, and clean and high-level disinfect or sterilize it according to Table C-11 (page C-43).

## Using A Gastric Tube For Feeding Or Drainage

### Using A Gastric Tube For Feeding Or Drainage — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the gastric tube is inserted for the purpose of giving expressed

breast milk, see page C-18 for instructions on feeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the gastric tube is inserted for drainage, leave the tube uncapped

and wrap clean gauze around the end, fastened with tape, to keep the tube clean and to absorb the drainage from the stomach.

## Performing A Lumbar Puncture P-37

### Performing A Lumbar Puncture P-37 — phần 1

Lumbar puncture is used to confirm the diagnosis when the baby has signs suggestive of meningitis. Do not perform a lumbar puncture if the baby has spina bifida/meningomyelocoele.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; high-level disinfected or sterile gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile drapes

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; swabs or cotton-wool balls soaked in antiseptic solution (Table C-10,

page C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; spinal needle or intravenous needle (22- to 24-gauge)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; appropriate collection tubes

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; dry cotton-wool ball

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; adhesive bandage

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Be prepared to resuscitate the baby using a bag and mask (page P-1), if

necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby under a radiant warmer (page C-5), if possible, and

undress the baby only when ready to perform the procedure.

### Performing A Lumbar Puncture P-37 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow principles of infection prevention (page C-37) and aseptic

technique (page C-40).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Position the baby:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have an assistant hold the baby in a sitting position:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Position the baby so that the baby’s legs are straight and the

back is arched (Fig. P-15, page P-38);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the baby’s neck is partially extended and not flexed

towards the chest, which could obstruct the baby’s airway.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-38; Performing a lumbar puncture

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-15; Sitting position for lumbar puncture

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Alternatively, place the baby on her/his side facing the assistant

(most right-handed health care providers find it easiest if the baby is on her/his left side; Fig. P-16):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Position the baby so that the baby’s back is closest to the side of

### Performing A Lumbar Puncture P-37 — phần 3

the table from which the lumbar puncture will be performed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have the assistant place one hand behind the baby’s head and

neck, and place the other hand behind the baby’s thighs to hold the spine in a flexed position;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the baby’s neck is partially extended and not flexed

towards the chest, which could obstruct the baby’s airway.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-16; Lying position for lumbar puncture

- Dòng dữ liệu: Performing a lumbar puncture; P-39

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the skin over the area of the lumbar spine and then the remainder

of the back by washing in an outward spiral motion with a swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic solution. Repeat two more times, using a new swab or cotton-wool ball each time, and allow to dry.

### Performing A Lumbar Puncture P-37 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the site of the puncture between the third and fourth lumbar

processes (i.e. on a line joining the iliac crests; Fig. P-17).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-17; Site of lumbar puncture

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove examination gloves and put on high-level disinfected or sterile

gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place sterile drapes over the baby’s body so that only the puncture site is

exposed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert the needle in the midline of the vertebrae, angled towards the

baby’s umbilicus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Slowly advance the needle to a depth of about 1 cm (or less if the baby is

small [less than 2.5 kg at birth or born before 37 weeks gestation]). A slight “pop” may be felt as the needle enters the subarachnoid space.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If using a spinal needle, remove the stylet.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bone is encountered, the needle cannot be redirected. Pull the needle

### Performing A Lumbar Puncture P-37 — phần 5

back to just beneath the skin and reinsert the needle, directing it slightly upward while aiming for the baby’s umbilicus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-40; Performing a lumbar puncture

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Collect the cerebrospinal fluid (CSF):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Collect about 0.5 to 1 ml (about 6 to 10 drops) of CSF in each

collection tube;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If CSF does not come out, rotate the needle slightly;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If CSF still does not come out, remove the needle and reinsert it

between the fourth and fifth lumbar processes;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If blood is seen in the CSF, the needle probably went through the

spinal canal and caused bleeding. If the CSF does not clear, collect enough CSF for culture and sensitivity only.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the CSF is collected, remove the needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have an assistant apply gentle pressure to the puncture site with a cotton-

### Performing A Lumbar Puncture P-37 — phần 6

wool ball until bleeding or leakage of fluid stops.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply an adhesive bandage to the site.

## Administering Rectal Paraldehyde P-41

### Administering Rectal Paraldehyde P-41 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; 3-ml plastic syringe

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 1-inch needle (22- to 23-gauge)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; paraldehyde

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; arachis (or vegetable) oil

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; water-based lubricant

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have an assistant remove the baby’s napkin and hold the baby on one

side, similar to the lying position for lumbar puncture (Fig. P-16, page P-38).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Draw up 2 ml arachis oil into the syringe.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Attach the needle and draw up the paraldehyde 0.3 ml/kg body weight

into the same syringe.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the needle from the syringe.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lubricate the syringe with a water-based lubricant.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently insert the syringe into the baby’s rectum and advance it

approximately 3 cm.

### Administering Rectal Paraldehyde P-41 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Administer the drug slowly over three minutes and then slowly

withdraw the syringe.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the baby to relax from the curled up position.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the dose is passed from the rectum within the first five minutes,

repeat the dose. The majority of absorption will occur between 5 and 15 minutes after administration, so if stool is passed after this, the dose does not need to be repeated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-42; Administering rectal paraldehyde

## Draining An Abscess P-43

### Draining An Abscess P-43 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean examination gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; high-level disinfected or sterile gloves

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; swab or cotton-wool ball soaked in antiseptic solution (Table C-10, page

C-41)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile swab in glass test tube for culture

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; high-level disinfected or sterile tissue forceps

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile gauze

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile blade

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile drapes

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 10-ml syringe

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 3-ml syringe (or other available size with adequate markings for

proper dose)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 22-gauge needle

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile 25-gauge, 5/8-inch needle

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; local anaesthetic (e.g. 0.5% lignocaine solution)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile IV fluid

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands (page C-38), and put on clean examination gloves.

### Draining An Abscess P-43 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the skin over and around the abscess using a swab or cotton-

wool ball soaked in antiseptic solution, and allow to dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove examination gloves and put on high-level disinfected or sterile

gloves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Attach the 22-gauge needle to the 10-ml syringe, fill the syringe with IV

fluid, and remove the needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place sterile drapes over the area surrounding the abscess so that only the

abscess is exposed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-44; Draining an abscess

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject local anaesthetic around the abscess:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Draw the local anaesthetic into the 3-ml syringe and attach the

25-gauge needle;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Inject local anaesthetic intradermally (page P-19) to raise a small

“bleb” in the skin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; After one minute, attach the 22-gauge needle to the syringe, insert

### Draining An Abscess P-43 — phần 3

the needle through the “bleb” in the skin, and infiltrate with local anaesthetic the area around the abscess.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After waiting two minutes to allow the local anaesthetic to take effect,

make an incision over the fluctuant area of the abscess.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Using a sterile swab, take a sample of the pus, and send it to the

laboratory for culture and sensitivity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use high-level disinfected or sterile tissue forceps to break up the pocket

of pus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Flush out the abscess with IV fluid, and leave the wound open.

## Record Keeping A-1

### Record Keeping A-1 — phần 1

There are several types of records used in the care of newborn babies. The following records are included in this guide: general clinical record, discharge form, referral form, feedback form, and death certificate. The example records included are suggested for their content more than for their format. Additional records may be used for administrative purposes. In addition, a log book (page A-8) should be kept with basic information on all babies admitted.

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; Ensure that the baby=s name and identification number are correct on all

records.

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; Store clinical records according to institutional policy. Ensure that the

records can be easily retrieved for the annual statistics or special investigations or studies.

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; Keep clinical records for at least one year unless directed otherwise by

local policy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; Be familiar with the requirements for country reporting of diseases

### Record Keeping A-1 — phần 2

under surveillance (e.g. congenital syphilis).

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; Analyse the hospital=s statistics every 6 or 12 months:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Number of babies admitted;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Number of babies discharged;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Number of babies who died;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Main conditions by birth weight and sex of the baby;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Shortages of drugs, equipment, supplies, and staff on duty.

## Clinical Record

### Clinical Record — phần 1

The clinical record consists of the admission record and record of ongoing care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; The admission record (example not shown) should include the reason

for admission, history, previous treatment, and other information about the mother and family. A checklist of tasks is useful.

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; The record of ongoing care (e.g. Fig. A-1, page A-2) during

hospitalization is a confidential record that is shared between the doctor and nurses involved in the baby=s care:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Keep the record near the baby;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use more than one column, if necessary, for daily recording when

the baby is ill or when the baby is recovering.

- Dòng dữ liệu: A-2; Record keeping

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE A-1; Clinical record of ongoing care

- Dòng dữ liệu: Record ID; Name and Sex; Birth Weight; Gestational Age; Diagnosis; Ward

Page #

- Dòng dữ liệu: Date; Day

Weight Temperature Breathing Feeding instructions

### Clinical Record — phần 2

Feeding (see key below)

Examination findings

Diagnostic tests

Treatment

Key for feeding: B = baby is breastfeeding. If the baby is not breastfeeding, indicate in the left column what the baby is fed (BM = expressed breast milk, S = breast-milk substitute) and the method of feeding (C = fed by cup/spoon or other device, T = fed by gastric tube), and indicate in the right column the volume of milk given.

- Dòng dữ liệu: A-3; Record keeping

## International Medical Certificate Of Cause Of Death

### International Medical Certificate Of Cause Of Death — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; If the baby dies, fill out a death certificate and send it to the authorities

according to national requirements. Use the International Classification of Disease or other coding system used in the country. Follow the instructions for coding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; Figure A-5 (page A-6) is an example of the death certificate proposed

by WHO. Each country has its own form and regulations for reporting a death and cause of death, which determine the content of the form and the time period within which the death must be reported. Consider adding additional information on the baby, if necessary, to the country death certificate.

## Log Book

### Log Book — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; Keep a log book with the basic information on all babies admitted:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; identification number;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; record ID;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; name;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; address;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; sex;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; condition at discharge (alive, transferred, died);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; next follow-up visit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: $; Minimal information includes date of admission, names of the mother

and the baby, main condition, outcome (discharged home, transferred, or died), and date. It is useful to include birth weight.

transferred, or required follow-up.

## Biochemistry And Haematology

### Biochemistry And Haematology — phần 1

Supplies necessary to: Measure blood glucose Measure haemoglobin (or haematocrit [erythrocyte volume fraction]) Measure serum bilirubin Perform cerebrospinal fluid cell count Perform serologic test for syphilis

## Microbiology

### Microbiology — phần 1

Supplies necessary to perform: Culture and sensitivity on samples of blood, pus, and cerebrospinal fluid Gram stain

## Blood Bank

### Blood Bank — phần 1

Fresh whole blood, including type O, Rh-negative blood Supplies necessary to perform: Blood type and cross-match Coombs test

## Non-Patient-Care Areas

### Non-Patient-Care Areas — phần 1

Area for preparing IV fluid and drugs Refrigerator and/or freezer Storage space for supplies Torch with extra batteries and bulb

## Patient-Care Areas

### Patient-Care Areas — phần 1

Blankets, bed linen, baby clothes (or cloth for wrapping), hats, and napkins Chair for mother (comfortable for breastfeeding) Cots for newborn babies Facilities for handwashing Heat source

- Dòng dữ liệu: A-10; Essential equipment, supplies, and drugs

Light source Oxygen supply (e.g. from a cylinder, oxygen concentrator, or wall outlet) and a flow metre allowing flow as low as 0.5 litres per minute Room thermometer Wall clock

## Equipment

### Equipment — phần 1

Face masks for resuscitation (sizes 0 and 1) Incubators IV poles Phototherapy unit Radiant warmers Self-inflating resuscitation bag, newborn-size Stethoscope Suction apparatus Thermometers (including a rectal thermometer that measures as low as 25 °C) Weighing scale with 5- to 10-g increments

## Tubes And Catheters

### Tubes And Catheters — phần 1

Gastric tubes (3.5-F, 5-F, and 8-F) with caps Suction catheters Umbilical vein catheter

## Methods Of Administering Oxygen

### Methods Of Administering Oxygen — phần 1

Head box Nasal catheter (6-F and 8-F) Nasal prongs (1-mm and 2-mm)

## Iv Line

### Iv Line — phần 1

Butterfly sets (22- to 25-gauge) Cannulas (22- to 25-gauge) IV tubing Microdropper Stopcocks (two-way or three-way)

## Injections

### Injections — phần 1

Needles (21- to 27-gauge) Syringes (1- to 10-ml)

## Instruments

### Instruments — phần 1

Blades and handles Forceps (e.g. artery, dissecting, tissue) Needle holder

## Tests

### Tests — phần 1

Capillary tubes Collection tubes appropriate for sample (blood, pus, and cerebrospinal fluid) Glucose paper reagent strips Lancet

## Feeding And Breastfeeding

### Feeding And Breastfeeding — phần 1
